# 당나귀귀 블로그 — 전체 소스코드 모음

| 항목 | 내용 |
|------|------|
| 프로젝트 | 당나귀귀 블로그 (생성형 AI 댓글 일기 / 인스타그램형 SNS) |
| 제출자 | 황채원 · 2316916 · 소프트웨어학부 데이터 사이언스 전공 |
| 구성 | ① 프론트엔드 SPA(`index.html`) ② 멀티유저 백엔드(`next/`) |
| 사용 모델 | OpenAI ChatGPT API (gpt-4o-mini · gpt-image-1) |

> 본 문서는 웹사이트 제작에 사용한 전체 소스코드를 파일 경로별로 정리한 코드 부록입니다.
> (자동 생성 파일 `node_modules/`, `.next/`, `package-lock.json`, `next-env.d.ts`는 제외했습니다.)

---

## 1. 프론트엔드 (단일 페이지 앱)

사용자가 직접 보는 화면·AI 호출·캐릭터 로직·localStorage 저장을 모두 담은 단일 HTML 파일입니다.

### `index.html`  _(4999 lines)_

```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="ko">
<head>
  <meta charset="UTF-8" />
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0, maximum-scale=1.0, minimum-scale=1.0, user-scalable=no, viewport-fit=cover" />
  <meta name="theme-color" content="#ffd6e8" />
  <meta name="apple-mobile-web-app-capable" content="yes" />
  <meta name="apple-mobile-web-app-status-bar-style" content="default" />
  <meta name="apple-mobile-web-app-title" content="Princessgram" />
  <meta name="format-detection" content="telephone=no" />
  <title>Princessgram 👑</title>
  <style>
    * { box-sizing: border-box; margin: 0; padding: 0; -webkit-touch-callout: none; }

    html { -webkit-text-size-adjust: 100%; }

    html, body {
      position: fixed;
      inset: 0;
      width: 100%;
      height: 100%;
      overflow: hidden;
      overscroll-behavior: none;
      touch-action: pan-y;
    }

    body {
      background: #fff0f5;
      font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, 'Segoe UI', sans-serif;
      display: flex;
      justify-content: center;
      -webkit-tap-highlight-color: transparent;
    }

    img { -webkit-user-drag: none; user-select: none; }

    button, a, [role="button"], input, textarea, select, label, .nav-btn, .story, .post-menu-btn { touch-action: manipulation; }

    .app {
      width: 100%;
      max-width: 390px;
      background: #fff;
      height: 100%;
      display: flex;
      flex-direction: column;
      overflow-y: auto;
      overflow-x: hidden;
      -webkit-overflow-scrolling: touch;
      overscroll-behavior: contain;
      box-shadow: 0 0 30px rgba(255,150,180,0.2);
      scrollbar-width: none;
      -ms-overflow-style: none;
    }
    .app::-webkit-scrollbar { display: none; }
    *::-webkit-scrollbar { display: none; }
    * { scrollbar-width: none; -ms-overflow-style: none; }

    /* ── Header ── */
    .header {
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, #ffe4ef 0%, #ffd6e8 100%);
      border-bottom: 1px solid #ffc5db;
      padding: calc(12px + env(safe-area-inset-top)) 16px 12px;
      display: flex;
      align-items: center;
      justify-content: space-between;
      position: sticky;
      top: 0;
      z-index: 100;
    }
    .header-logo {
      font-size: 22px;
      font-weight: 700;
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, #d4548a, #e8a0bf);
      -webkit-background-clip: text;
      -webkit-text-fill-color: transparent;
      letter-spacing: -0.5px;
      font-style: italic;
    }
    .header-icons {
      display: flex;
      gap: 16px;
      font-size: 20px;
      color: #c06090;
    }

    /* ── Stories ── */
    .stories {
      display: flex;
      gap: 12px;
      padding: 12px 16px;
      overflow-x: auto;
      border-bottom: 1px solid #ffe4ef;
      scrollbar-width: none;
      touch-action: pan-x;
      overscroll-behavior-x: contain;
    }
    .stories::-webkit-scrollbar { display: none; }
    .story {
      display: flex;
      flex-direction: column;
      align-items: center;
      gap: 4px;
      cursor: pointer;
      flex-shrink: 0;
    }
    .story-ring {
      width: 64px;
      height: 64px;
      border-radius: 50%;
      background: linear-gradient(45deg, #f9a8d4, #ec4899, #db2777);
      padding: 2.5px;
    }
    .story-ring.mine {
      background: linear-gradient(45deg, #fbbf24, #f59e0b);
    }
    .story-avatar {
      width: 100%;
      height: 100%;
      border-radius: 50%;
      border: 2px solid #fff;
      display: flex;
      align-items: center;
      justify-content: center;
      font-size: 26px;
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, #ffe4ef, #ffd6e8);
      overflow: hidden;
    }
    .story-avatar img {
      width: 100%;
      height: 100%;
      object-fit: cover;
      border-radius: 50%;
    }
    .comment-avatar img {
      width: 100%;
      height: 100%;
      object-fit: cover;
      border-radius: 50%;
    }
    .comment-avatar {
      overflow: hidden;
    }
    .story-name {
      font-size: 11px;
      color: #666;
      max-width: 66px;
      text-align: center;
      overflow: hidden;
      text-overflow: ellipsis;
      white-space: nowrap;
    }

    /* ── Write Box ── */
    .write-box {
      margin: 16px;
      border: 1.5px solid #ffc5db;
      border-radius: 16px;
      overflow: hidden;
      background: #fff9fc;
    }
    .write-header {
      display: flex;
      align-items: center;
      gap: 10px;
      padding: 12px 14px 8px;
    }
    .write-avatar {
      width: 36px;
      height: 36px;
      border-radius: 50%;
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, #fda4af, #f43f5e);
      display: flex;
      align-items: center;
      justify-content: center;
      font-size: 18px;
    }
    .write-name {
      font-weight: 600;
      font-size: 13px;
      color: #333;
    }
    .write-sub {
      font-size: 11px;
      color: #aaa;
    }
    textarea {
      width: 100%;
      border: none;
      outline: none;
      resize: none;
      padding: 0 14px 12px;
      font-size: 14px;
      color: #333;
      background: transparent;
      line-height: 1.6;
      min-height: 100px;
      font-family: inherit;
    }
    textarea::placeholder { color: #ccc; }
    .write-footer {
      display: flex;
      align-items: center;
      justify-content: space-between;
      padding: 10px 14px;
      border-top: 1px solid #ffe4ef;
    }
    .write-emojis { font-size: 18px; display: flex; gap: 8px; }
    .post-btn {
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, #ec4899, #db2777);
      color: #fff;
      border: none;
      border-radius: 20px;
      padding: 7px 20px;
      font-size: 13px;
      font-weight: 600;
      cursor: pointer;
      transition: opacity 0.2s;
    }
    .post-btn:hover { opacity: 0.85; }
    .post-btn:disabled { opacity: 0.5; cursor: not-allowed; }

    .comment-send-btn {
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, #f9a8d4, #f472b6);
      color: #fff;
      border: none;
      border-radius: 50%;
      width: 28px;
      height: 28px;
      display: inline-flex;
      align-items: center;
      justify-content: center;
      cursor: pointer;
      padding: 0;
      flex-shrink: 0;
      transition: transform 0.1s, opacity 0.2s;
    }
    .comment-send-btn:hover { opacity: 0.9; }
    .comment-send-btn:active { transform: scale(0.9); }
    .comment-send-btn svg { width: 14px; height: 14px; stroke: #fff; }

    /* ── Divider ── */
    .divider {
      height: 1px;
      background: #ffe4ef;
      margin: 0 16px 4px;
    }

    /* ── Feed ── */
    .feed { flex: 1; padding: 8px 0 calc(80px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom)); overscroll-behavior: contain; }

    .post-card {
      margin-bottom: 20px;
    }

    .post-top {
      display: flex;
      align-items: center;
      gap: 10px;
      padding: 10px 16px;
    }
    .post-avatar {
      width: 36px;
      height: 36px;
      border-radius: 50%;
      display: flex;
      align-items: center;
      justify-content: center;
      font-size: 18px;
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, #fda4af, #f43f5e);
      flex-shrink: 0;
    }
    .post-info { flex: 1; }
    .post-username { font-weight: 600; font-size: 13px; color: #222; }
    .post-time { font-size: 11px; color: #aaa; }

    .post-content {
      padding: 0 16px 12px;
      font-size: 14px;
      color: #333;
      line-height: 1.7;
      white-space: pre-wrap;
      word-break: break-word;
    }

    .post-image {
      width: 100%;
      aspect-ratio: 1;
      object-fit: cover;
      display: block;
    }

    /* castle placeholder */
    .post-image-placeholder {
      width: 100%;
      aspect-ratio: 1;
      background: linear-gradient(160deg, #ffdde8 0%, #ffb6c9 40%, #ff8fab 100%);
      display: flex;
      align-items: center;
      justify-content: center;
      font-size: 80px;
    }

    .post-actions {
      display: flex;
      align-items: center;
      gap: 14px;
      padding: 10px 16px 6px;
    }
    .action-btn {
      background: none;
      border: none;
      cursor: pointer;
      font-size: 22px;
      line-height: 1;
      transition: transform 0.1s;
    }
    .action-btn:active { transform: scale(1.3); }
    .action-btn.liked { filter: none; }

    .likes-count {
      padding: 0 16px 4px;
      font-size: 13px;
      font-weight: 600;
      color: #333;
    }

    /* ── Comments ── */
    .comments { padding: 0 16px 10px; }
    .comment {
      display: flex;
      gap: 8px;
      margin-bottom: 8px;
      animation: fadeIn 0.4s ease;
    }
    @keyframes fadeIn {
      from { opacity: 0; transform: translateY(6px); }
      to   { opacity: 1; transform: translateY(0); }
    }
    .comment-avatar {
      width: 28px;
      height: 28px;
      border-radius: 50%;
      display: flex;
      align-items: center;
      justify-content: center;
      font-size: 14px;
      flex-shrink: 0;
    }
    .comment-body { flex: 1; }
    .comment-name { font-weight: 600; font-size: 12px; color: #222; }
    .comment-text { font-size: 13px; color: #444; line-height: 1.5; }
    .comment-time { font-size: 10px; color: #bbb; margin-top: 2px; }
    .comment-delete {
      font-size: 10px; color: #ccc; cursor: pointer;
      margin-left: 8px; font-weight: 500;
      transition: color 0.15s;
    }
    .comment-delete:hover { color: #ec4899; }
    .reply-btn {
      background: none; border: none; color: #b08099;
      font-size: 11px; padding: 2px 0; margin-top: 2px;
      cursor: pointer; font-weight: 600;
    }
    .reply-btn:hover { color: #ec4899; }
    .reply-input-wrap {
      display: none; gap: 6px; margin: 6px 0 4px 36px;
    }
    .reply-input-wrap.open { display: flex; }
    .reply-input-wrap input {
      flex: 1; border: 1px solid #ffd6e8; border-radius: 16px;
      padding: 6px 12px; font-size: 12px; outline: none;
    }
    .reply-input-wrap input:focus { border-color: #ec4899; }
    .reply-input-wrap button {
      background: #ec4899; color: #fff; border: none;
      border-radius: 14px; padding: 4px 12px; font-size: 12px;
      cursor: pointer; font-weight: 600;
    }
    .replies { margin-left: 36px; margin-top: 4px; }
    .reply { display: flex; gap: 8px; margin-bottom: 6px; animation: fadeIn 0.4s ease; }
    .reply .comment-avatar { width: 22px; height: 22px; }
    .reply .comment-name { font-size: 11px; }
    .reply .comment-text { font-size: 12px; }
    .reply.me .comment-name { color: #ec4899; }

    /* ── DM / Messenger (Instagram-style) ── */
    .nav-badge {
      position: absolute; top: 4px; right: calc(50% - 22px);
      background: #ec4899; color: #fff; font-size: 10px;
      min-width: 16px; height: 16px; padding: 0 4px;
      border-radius: 8px; display: none;
      align-items: center; justify-content: center; font-weight: 700;
    }
    .nav-badge.show { display: flex; }

    /* DM list header (인스타 inbox 헤더) */
    .dm-list-header {
      display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between;
      padding: 12px 16px 8px;
      border-bottom: 1px solid #efefef;
      background: #fff;
    }
    .dm-list-header-title {
      font-size: 16px; font-weight: 700; color: #262626;
      display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 6px;
    }
    .dm-list-header-icon {
      font-size: 22px; color: #262626; cursor: pointer;
      background: none; border: none; padding: 4px;
    }
    .dm-people-row {
      display: flex; gap: 14px;
      padding: 14px 16px 16px;
      overflow-x: auto;
      background: #fff;
      border-bottom: 1px solid #efefef;
      touch-action: pan-x;
      overscroll-behavior-x: contain;
    }
    .dm-people-row::-webkit-scrollbar { display: none; }
    .dm-person {
      display: flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: center;
      gap: 6px; cursor: pointer; flex-shrink: 0;
      width: 64px;
    }
    .dm-person-avatar {
      width: 56px; height: 56px; border-radius: 50%;
      overflow: hidden;
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, #fda4af 0%, #f9a8d4 100%);
      display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center;
      font-size: 28px;
      border: 2px solid #fff;
      box-shadow: 0 0 0 1.5px #ffc5db;
    }
    .dm-person-avatar img { width: 100%; height: 100%; object-fit: cover; }
    .dm-person-name {
      font-size: 11px; color: #262626;
      max-width: 64px; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis;
      white-space: nowrap; text-align: center;
    }
    .dm-list-tabs {
      display: flex; padding: 8px 16px; gap: 16px;
      font-size: 14px; font-weight: 600;
      border-bottom: 1px solid #efefef;
      background: #fff;
    }
    .dm-list-tab {
      padding: 6px 0; cursor: pointer; color: #8e8e8e;
      border-bottom: 2px solid transparent;
    }
    .dm-list-tab.active { color: #262626; border-bottom-color: #262626; }

    .dm-list { width: 100%; padding: 4px 0 calc(100px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom)); background: #fff; overscroll-behavior: contain; }
    .dm-list-empty {
      text-align: center; color: #8e8e8e; font-size: 14px;
      padding: 80px 24px;
      line-height: 1.6;
    }
    .dm-thread-item {
      display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 12px;
      padding: 8px 16px; cursor: pointer;
      transition: background 0.12s;
      background: #fff;
    }
    .dm-thread-item:hover { background: #fafafa; }
    .dm-thread-item:active { background: #f0f0f0; }
    .dm-thread-avatar {
      width: 56px; height: 56px; border-radius: 50%;
      overflow: hidden; flex-shrink: 0;
      display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center;
      font-size: 28px;
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, #fda4af 0%, #f9a8d4 100%);
    }
    .dm-thread-avatar img { width: 100%; height: 100%; object-fit: cover; }
    .dm-thread-meta { flex: 1; min-width: 0; text-align: left; }
    .dm-thread-name {
      font-weight: 600; font-size: 14px; color: #262626;
      display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 6px;
    }
    .dm-thread-preview {
      font-size: 14px; color: #8e8e8e; margin-top: 2px;
      word-break: break-word;
      line-height: 1.4;
    }
    .dm-thread-preview .preview-sep { color: #8e8e8e; margin: 0 4px; }
    .dm-thread-preview .preview-sep { color: #8e8e8e; margin: 0 2px; }
    .dm-thread-preview.unread { color: #262626; font-weight: 600; }
    .dm-thread-right {
      display: flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: flex-end;
      gap: 4px; flex-shrink: 0;
    }
    .dm-unread-dot {
      width: 10px; height: 10px; border-radius: 50%;
      background: #0095f6;
    }
    .dm-thread-camera {
      font-size: 22px; color: #262626; cursor: pointer;
    }
    .dm-thread-delete {
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, #ffe4ef, #ffd6e8);
      border: 1px solid #ffc5db;
      color: #d4548a;
      width: 32px; height: 32px;
      border-radius: 50%;
      display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center;
      cursor: pointer;
      font-size: 14px; font-weight: 600;
      box-shadow: 0 1px 3px rgba(236, 72, 153, 0.15);
      transition: all 0.15s;
    }
    .dm-thread-delete:hover {
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, #ffd0e3, #ffb6cd);
      transform: scale(1.08);
      box-shadow: 0 2px 6px rgba(236, 72, 153, 0.25);
    }
    .dm-thread-delete:active { transform: scale(0.95); }

    /* DM 채팅 뷰 */
    .dm-chat {
      display: none; flex-direction: column;
      flex: 1; width: 100%; min-height: 0;
      background: #fff;
    }
    .dm-chat.open { display: flex; }
    .dm-chat-header {
      display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 12px;
      padding: 10px 14px;
      border-bottom: 1px solid #efefef;
      background: #fff;
      position: sticky; top: 0; z-index: 10;
    }
    .dm-chat-back {
      background: none; border: none; font-size: 24px;
      color: #262626; cursor: pointer; padding: 0 4px;
      line-height: 1;
    }
    .dm-chat-avatar-wrap {
      display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px;
      flex: 1; min-width: 0;
    }
    .dm-chat-avatar {
      width: 36px; height: 36px; border-radius: 50%;
      overflow: hidden; flex-shrink: 0;
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, #fda4af 0%, #f9a8d4 100%);
      display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center;
      font-size: 20px;
    }
    .dm-chat-avatar img { width: 100%; height: 100%; object-fit: cover; }
    .dm-chat-name-wrap { display: flex; flex-direction: column; min-width: 0; }
    .dm-chat-name {
      font-weight: 600; font-size: 15px; color: #262626;
      line-height: 1.2;
    }
    .dm-chat-status {
      font-size: 11px; color: #8e8e8e; line-height: 1.2;
    }
    .dm-chat-actions {
      display: flex; gap: 14px; align-items: center;
      font-size: 22px; color: #262626;
    }
    .dm-chat-actions span { cursor: pointer; }

    /* 상단 프로필 (인스타: 아바타 + 이름 + 프로필 보기) */
    .dm-chat-profile-card {
      display: flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: center;
      padding: 24px 16px 16px;
      background: #fff;
    }
    .dm-chat-profile-card .pc-avatar {
      width: 88px; height: 88px; border-radius: 50%;
      overflow: hidden; margin-bottom: 12px;
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, #fda4af 0%, #f9a8d4 100%);
      display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center;
      font-size: 44px;
    }
    .dm-chat-profile-card .pc-avatar img {
      width: 100%; height: 100%; object-fit: cover;
    }
    .dm-chat-profile-card .pc-name {
      font-size: 20px; font-weight: 600; color: #262626;
      margin-bottom: 4px;
    }
    .dm-chat-profile-card .pc-username {
      font-size: 13px; color: #8e8e8e; margin-bottom: 14px;
    }
    .dm-chat-profile-card .pc-view-btn {
      background: #efefef; color: #262626;
      border: none; padding: 7px 16px;
      border-radius: 8px; font-size: 13px; font-weight: 600;
      cursor: pointer;
    }
    .dm-chat-profile-card .pc-view-btn:hover { background: #dbdbdb; }

    .dm-messages {
      flex: 1; overflow-y: auto;
      padding: 8px 12px 8px;
      display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 2px;
      background: #fff;
    }
    .dm-msg-row {
      display: flex; align-items: flex-end; gap: 6px;
      margin: 1px 0;
    }
    .dm-msg-row.me { justify-content: flex-end; }
    .dm-msg-row.them { justify-content: flex-start; }
    .dm-msg-row-avatar {
      width: 28px; height: 28px; border-radius: 50%;
      overflow: hidden; flex-shrink: 0;
      display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center;
      font-size: 14px;
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, #fda4af 0%, #f9a8d4 100%);
      align-self: flex-end;
      margin-bottom: 2px;
    }
    .dm-msg-row-avatar img { width: 100%; height: 100%; object-fit: cover; }
    .dm-msg-row-avatar.hidden { visibility: hidden; }
    .dm-msg {
      max-width: 70%; padding: 9px 13px;
      border-radius: 22px; font-size: 14px;
      line-height: 1.35; word-break: break-word;
      animation: fadeIn 0.25s ease;
    }
    .dm-msg.them {
      background: #efefef; color: #262626;
    }
    .dm-msg.me {
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, #ec4899, #f472b6);
      color: #fff;
    }
    /* 같은 사람이 연속 메시지 보낼 때 모서리 조절 */
    .dm-msg.them.has-prev { border-top-left-radius: 6px; }
    .dm-msg.them.has-next { border-bottom-left-radius: 6px; }
    .dm-msg.me.has-prev { border-top-right-radius: 6px; }
    .dm-msg.me.has-next { border-bottom-right-radius: 6px; }

    .dm-msg-divider {
      text-align: center;
      font-size: 11px; color: #8e8e8e;
      padding: 16px 0 8px;
      font-weight: 600;
    }

    .dm-input-bar {
      position: sticky; bottom: 0;
      display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px;
      padding: 8px 12px 10px;
      background: #fff;
      border-top: 1px solid #efefef;
    }
    .dm-input-wrap {
      flex: 1;
      display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px;
      border: 1px solid #dbdbdb; border-radius: 22px;
      padding: 6px 6px 6px 14px;
      transition: border-color 0.15s;
    }
    .dm-input-wrap:focus-within { border-color: #a8a8a8; }
    .dm-input-wrap input {
      flex: 1; border: none; outline: none;
      font-size: 14px; background: transparent;
      padding: 4px 0;
    }
    .dm-input-emoji {
      background: none; border: none; font-size: 18px;
      cursor: pointer; padding: 4px;
    }
    .dm-input-send {
      background: none; color: #0095f6;
      border: none; padding: 4px 8px;
      font-size: 14px; font-weight: 600; cursor: pointer;
      display: none;
    }
    .dm-input-send.show { display: block; }
    .dm-input-actions {
      display: flex; gap: 12px; align-items: center;
      font-size: 22px; color: #262626;
    }
    .dm-input-actions span { cursor: pointer; }

    .typing-indicator {
      display: flex;
      align-items: center;
      gap: 8px;
      padding: 4px 0 8px;
      color: #bbb;
      font-size: 12px;
      animation: fadeIn 0.3s ease;
    }
    .typing-dots { display: flex; gap: 3px; }
    .typing-dots span {
      width: 5px; height: 5px;
      border-radius: 50%;
      background: #f9a8d4;
      animation: bounce 1.2s infinite;
    }
    .typing-dots span:nth-child(2) { animation-delay: 0.2s; }
    .typing-dots span:nth-child(3) { animation-delay: 0.4s; }
    @keyframes bounce {
      0%, 60%, 100% { transform: translateY(0); }
      30% { transform: translateY(-5px); }
    }

    .post-border { border-bottom: 1px solid #ffe4ef; }

    /* ── Bottom Nav ── */
    body.dm-chat-open .bottom-nav { display: none; }
    body.dm-chat-open .messenger-page { padding-bottom: 0; }
    body.dm-chat-open { overflow: hidden; }
    body.dm-chat-open .app {
      height: 100vh;
      height: 100dvh;
      max-height: 100vh;
      max-height: 100dvh;
      min-height: 100vh;
      min-height: 100dvh;
      overflow: hidden;
    }
    .bottom-nav {
      position: fixed;
      bottom: 0;
      left: 50%;
      transform: translateX(-50%);
      width: 100%;
      max-width: 390px;
      background: rgba(255,255,255,0.95);
      border-top: 1px solid #ffe4ef;
      display: flex;
      justify-content: space-around;
      padding: 10px 0 calc(16px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom));
      backdrop-filter: blur(10px);
    }
    .nav-btn {
      background: none;
      border: none;
      cursor: pointer;
      padding: 6px 14px;
      color: #c06090;
      display: flex;
      align-items: center;
      justify-content: center;
      transition: transform 0.1s, color 0.15s;
    }
    .nav-btn:active { transform: scale(1.15); }
    .nav-btn svg { width: 26px; height: 26px; stroke: currentColor; fill: none; stroke-width: 2; stroke-linecap: round; stroke-linejoin: round; }
    .nav-btn.home { color: #d4548a; }

    /* ── Post menu ── */
    .post-menu-btn {
      background: none;
      border: none;
      cursor: pointer;
      color: #c06090;
      font-size: 20px;
      padding: 4px 8px;
      line-height: 1;
    }
    .post-menu {
      position: absolute;
      top: 38px;
      right: 12px;
      background: #fff;
      border: 1px solid #ffe4ef;
      border-radius: 12px;
      box-shadow: 0 8px 24px rgba(255,150,180,0.25);
      display: none;
      flex-direction: column;
      min-width: 130px;
      overflow: hidden;
      z-index: 50;
    }
    .post-menu.open { display: flex; }
    .post-menu button {
      background: none;
      border: none;
      padding: 10px 14px;
      font-size: 13px;
      text-align: left;
      cursor: pointer;
      color: #444;
      font-family: inherit;
    }
    .post-menu button:hover { background: #fff0f5; }
    .post-menu button.danger { color: #db2777; }
    .post-top { position: relative; }
    .pinned-label {
      display: inline-block;
      font-size: 10px;
      color: #d4548a;
      background: #ffe4ef;
      padding: 2px 6px;
      border-radius: 8px;
      margin-left: 6px;
      vertical-align: middle;
    }

    /* ── Messenger page ── */
    .messenger-page {
      flex: 1;
      display: none;
      flex-direction: column;
      padding: 0;
      color: #c06090;
      overflow: hidden;
      min-height: 0;
    }
    .messenger-page #dmListView {
      flex: 1; overflow-y: auto;
      display: flex; flex-direction: column;
    }
    .messenger-page #dmListView .dm-list { flex: 1; }
    .messenger-page .placeholder-empty {
      text-align: center;
      padding: 60px 20px;
    }
    .messenger-page.open { display: flex; }
    .messenger-page .icon { font-size: 60px; margin-bottom: 12px; }
    .messenger-page h2 { font-size: 18px; margin-bottom: 8px; color: #d4548a; }
    .messenger-page p { font-size: 13px; color: #aaa; }

    /* ── Profile page ── */
    .profile-page {
      flex: 1;
      display: none;
      flex-direction: column;
      padding: 24px 20px 100px;
    }
    .profile-page.open { display: flex; }
    .profile-avatar-wrap {
      align-self: center;
      width: 110px; height: 110px;
      border-radius: 50%;
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, #ffe4ef, #ffd6e8);
      border: 3px solid #fff;
      box-shadow: 0 6px 18px rgba(236,72,153,0.25);
      display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center;
      font-size: 56px; cursor: pointer; overflow: hidden;
      position: relative;
    }
    .profile-avatar-wrap img {
      width: 100%; height: 100%; object-fit: cover; border-radius: 50%;
    }
    .profile-avatar-edit {
      position: absolute; bottom: 0; right: 0;
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, #ec4899, #db2777);
      color: #fff; font-size: 13px;
      width: 30px; height: 30px; border-radius: 50%;
      display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center;
      box-shadow: 0 2px 8px rgba(219,39,119,0.4);
    }
    .profile-avatar-hint {
      text-align: center; font-size: 11px; color: #c06090;
      margin-top: 8px; margin-bottom: 18px;
    }
    .profile-field { margin-bottom: 14px; }
    .profile-field label {
      display: block; font-size: 12px; color: #888; margin-bottom: 6px;
      font-weight: 600;
    }
    .profile-field input, .profile-field select {
      width: 100%; padding: 10px 12px; border: 1.5px solid #ffc5db;
      border-radius: 10px; font-size: 13px; outline: none;
      font-family: inherit; background: #fff9fc; color: #333;
    }
    .profile-field input:focus, .profile-field select:focus { border-color: #ec4899; }
    .profile-field input[readonly] { background: #fff0f5; color: #888; }
    .ipcode-row { display: flex; gap: 6px; }
    .ipcode-row input { flex: 1; letter-spacing: 1px; font-family: monospace; font-weight: 600; }
    .copy-btn {
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, #fda4af, #f43f5e);
      color: #fff; border: none; padding: 0 14px;
      border-radius: 10px; cursor: pointer; font-size: 13px;
    }
    .profile-readonly {
      background: #fff0f5; padding: 10px 12px; border-radius: 10px;
      font-size: 13px; color: #c06090; border: 1.5px dashed #ffc5db;
    }
    .profile-actions {
      display: flex; gap: 8px; margin-top: 18px;
    }
    .profile-actions button {
      flex: 1; padding: 12px; border: none; border-radius: 12px;
      font-size: 14px; font-weight: 700; cursor: pointer;
      font-family: inherit;
    }
    .profile-actions .save {
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, #ec4899, #db2777); color: #fff;
    }
    .profile-actions .cancel {
      background: #fce7f3; color: #be185d;
    }
    .profile-page h2 {
      font-size: 18px; color: #d4548a; text-align: center; margin-bottom: 18px;
    }

    /* ── IG-style profile view ── */
    .profile-view { padding: 0 0 100px !important; }
    .ig-profile-topbar {
      display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between;
      padding: 12px 16px; border-bottom: 1px solid #ffe4ef;
    }
    .ig-profile-username { font-weight: 700; font-size: 16px; color: #222; }
    .ig-profile-settings { cursor: pointer; font-size: 20px; color: #c06090; }
    .ig-profile-head { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 24px; padding: 18px 20px 8px; }
    .ig-profile-avatar {
      width: 86px; height: 86px; border-radius: 50%;
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, #ffe4ef, #ffd6e8);
      display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center;
      font-size: 42px; overflow: hidden; flex-shrink: 0;
      border: 2px solid #fff; box-shadow: 0 2px 8px rgba(236,72,153,0.18);
    }
    .ig-profile-avatar img { width: 100%; height: 100%; object-fit: cover; }
    .ig-profile-stats { display: flex; gap: 22px; flex: 1; justify-content: space-around; }
    .ig-stat { text-align: center; }
    .ig-stat-num { font-weight: 700; font-size: 17px; color: #222; }
    .ig-stat-label { font-size: 12px; color: #777; margin-top: 2px; }
    .ig-profile-bio { padding: 4px 20px 10px 29px; font-size: 13px; color: #333; line-height: 1.5; }
    .ig-profile-name { font-weight: 700; margin-bottom: 4px; }
    .ig-profile-ipcode { font-family: monospace; font-size: 12px; color: #c06090; display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; margin-bottom: 4px; letter-spacing: 0.5px; }
    .ig-profile-desc { font-size: 12px; color: #888; }
    .ig-profile-actions { display: flex; gap: 8px; padding: 4px 20px 14px; }
    .ig-btn {
      flex: 1; padding: 8px; border: 1px solid #ffc5db;
      background: #fff9fc; border-radius: 10px;
      font-size: 13px; font-weight: 600; color: #c06090;
      cursor: pointer; font-family: inherit;
    }
    .ig-btn:disabled { opacity: 0.45; cursor: default; }
    .ig-profile-tabs {
      display: flex; border-top: 1px solid #ffe4ef;
    }
    .ig-tab {
      flex: 1; text-align: center; padding: 12px;
      font-size: 13px; color: #aaa; cursor: pointer;
      border-top: 2px solid transparent;
    }
    .ig-tab.active { color: #d4548a; border-top-color: #d4548a; font-weight: 600; }
    .ig-profile-grid {
      display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr); gap: 2px;
      padding: 2px;
    }
    .ig-grid-item {
      aspect-ratio: 1; background: linear-gradient(160deg, #ffdde8, #ffb6c9);
      cursor: pointer; overflow: hidden; position: relative;
    }
    .ig-grid-item img { width: 100%; height: 100%; object-fit: cover; display: block; }
    .ig-grid-item .grid-text {
      position: absolute; inset: 0; padding: 8px; font-size: 11px;
      color: #fff; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center;
      text-align: center; line-height: 1.4; overflow: hidden;
      text-shadow: 0 1px 2px rgba(0,0,0,0.15);
    }
    .ig-profile-empty {
      text-align: center; padding: 40px 20px; color: #c06090; font-size: 13px;
      display: none;
    }
    .ig-profile-empty.show { display: block; }

    /* ── Post image preview in write box ── */
    .post-image-preview {
      position: relative; margin: 0 14px 8px;
      border-radius: 10px; overflow: hidden;
      display: none;
    }
    .post-image-preview.show { display: block; }
    .post-image-preview img { width: 100%; max-height: 200px; object-fit: cover; display: block; }
    .post-image-preview .remove-btn {
      position: absolute; top: 6px; right: 6px;
      background: rgba(0,0,0,0.6); color: #fff; border: none;
      width: 24px; height: 24px; border-radius: 50%; cursor: pointer;
      font-size: 14px; line-height: 1;
    }
    .post-image-placeholder-ai {
      width: 100%; aspect-ratio: 1;
      background: linear-gradient(160deg, #ffdde8, #ffb6c9, #ff8fab);
      display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center;
      color: #fff; font-size: 14px; font-weight: 600;
      text-shadow: 0 1px 2px rgba(0,0,0,0.15);
    }

    /* ── Toast ── */
    .toast {
      position: fixed;
      bottom: 90px;
      left: 50%;
      transform: translateX(-50%) translateY(20px);
      background: rgba(219,39,119,0.95);
      color: #fff;
      padding: 10px 18px;
      border-radius: 20px;
      font-size: 13px;
      font-weight: 600;
      box-shadow: 0 8px 20px rgba(219,39,119,0.4);
      opacity: 0;
      pointer-events: none;
      transition: opacity 0.25s, transform 0.25s;
      z-index: 3000;
    }
    .toast.show { opacity: 1; transform: translateX(-50%) translateY(0); }

    /* ── Empty state ── */
    .empty-state {
      text-align: center;
      padding: 40px 20px;
      color: #daa;
    }
    .empty-state .icon { font-size: 50px; margin-bottom: 10px; }
    .empty-state p { font-size: 14px; }

    /* ── Settings Modal ── */
    .settings-overlay {
      position: fixed; inset: 0; background: rgba(0,0,0,0.5);
      display: none; align-items: center; justify-content: center;
      z-index: 2000;
    }
    .settings-overlay.open { display: flex; }
    .settings-box {
      background: #fff; border-radius: 18px; padding: 22px;
      width: 320px; max-width: 90%;
      box-shadow: 0 20px 50px rgba(0,0,0,0.25);
    }
    .settings-title {
      font-size: 17px; font-weight: 700; margin-bottom: 4px;
      color: #d4548a;
    }
    .settings-status {
      font-size: 12px; margin-bottom: 14px; color: #888;
    }
    .settings-status .badge {
      display: inline-block; padding: 2px 8px; border-radius: 10px;
      font-weight: 600; font-size: 11px;
    }
    .settings-status .badge.api { background: #d1fae5; color: #065f46; }
    .settings-status .badge.tmpl { background: #fee2e2; color: #991b1b; }
    .settings-box label {
      display: block; font-size: 12px; color: #666; margin-bottom: 6px;
    }
    .settings-box input {
      width: 100%; padding: 10px 12px; border: 1.5px solid #ffc5db;
      border-radius: 10px; font-size: 13px; outline: none;
      font-family: inherit;
    }
    .settings-box input:focus { border-color: #ec4899; }
    .settings-note {
      font-size: 11px; color: #aaa; margin-top: 8px; line-height: 1.5;
    }
    .settings-actions {
      display: flex; gap: 8px; margin-top: 16px;
    }
    .settings-btn {
      flex: 1; padding: 10px; border: none; border-radius: 10px;
      font-size: 13px; font-weight: 600; cursor: pointer;
      font-family: inherit;
    }
    .settings-btn.save {
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, #ec4899, #db2777); color: #fff;
    }
    .settings-btn.delete {
      background: #f3f4f6; color: #6b7280;
    }
    .settings-btn.close {
      background: #fce7f3; color: #be185d;
    }

    /* ── Mobile (phone screens): fill viewport edge-to-edge ── */
    @media (max-width: 500px) {
      .app { max-width: 100%; box-shadow: none; }
      .bottom-nav { max-width: 100%; left: 0; transform: none; }
      input, textarea, select, [contenteditable] { font-size: 16px; }
    }

    /* ── Invite Code Modal ── */
    .invite-modal-backdrop {
      position: fixed; inset: 0; background: rgba(60,30,50,0.55);
      backdrop-filter: blur(4px); display: none; align-items: center; justify-content: center;
      z-index: 4000; padding: 16px;
    }
    .invite-modal-backdrop.show { display: flex; }
    .invite-modal {
      width: 100%; max-width: 340px; background: linear-gradient(160deg, #fff, #fff4f9);
      border-radius: 20px; padding: 22px 22px 18px; text-align: center;
      box-shadow: 0 20px 60px rgba(236,72,153,0.25);
    }
    .invite-modal h3 { margin: 0 0 6px; font-size: 16px; color: #c0306a; font-weight: 700; }
    .invite-modal p.sub { margin: 0 0 14px; font-size: 12px; color: #b07090; line-height: 1.5; }
    .invite-modal input.code {
      width: 100%; box-sizing: border-box; padding: 12px; font-family: ui-monospace, monospace;
      font-size: 20px; letter-spacing: 6px; text-align: center; text-transform: uppercase;
      border: 1.5px solid #f9a8d4; border-radius: 12px; outline: none; background: #fff;
      color: #c0306a; font-weight: 700;
    }
    .invite-modal input.code:focus { border-color: #ec4899; box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px rgba(236,72,153,0.15); }
    .invite-modal .msg { min-height: 16px; margin-top: 8px; font-size: 12px; color: #c0306a; }
    .invite-modal .msg.error { color: #b91c5c; }
    .invite-modal .actions { display: flex; gap: 8px; margin-top: 14px; }
    .invite-modal .actions button {
      flex: 1; padding: 10px; border-radius: 10px; border: none; cursor: pointer;
      font-weight: 700; font-size: 13px;
    }
    .invite-modal .actions .cancel { background: #fdecf3; color: #c0306a; }
    .invite-modal .actions .submit {
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, #f9a8d4, #ec4899); color: #fff;
    }
    .invite-modal .actions .submit:disabled { opacity: 0.5; cursor: not-allowed; }

    /* ── Invite Success Burst ── */
    .invite-burst {
      position: fixed; inset: 0; pointer-events: none; z-index: 5000;
      display: none; align-items: center; justify-content: center;
    }
    .invite-burst.show { display: flex; }
    .invite-burst .ib-core {
      width: 120px; height: 120px; border-radius: 50%;
      background: radial-gradient(circle at 30% 30%, #fff, #f9a8d4 70%, #ec4899);
      display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center;
      font-size: 60px; box-shadow: 0 0 80px rgba(236,72,153,0.5);
      animation: ibPop 1.6s ease-out forwards;
      position: relative; overflow: hidden;
    }
    .invite-burst .ib-core img { width: 100%; height: 100%; object-fit: cover; border-radius: 50%; }
    .invite-burst .ib-text {
      position: absolute; top: 62%; left: 50%; transform: translateX(-50%);
      font-size: 13px; font-weight: 700; color: #c0306a; text-align: center;
      white-space: nowrap; background: rgba(255,255,255,0.85);
      padding: 8px 14px; border-radius: 18px;
      animation: ibFade 1.6s ease-out forwards;
      box-shadow: 0 4px 16px rgba(236,72,153,0.2);
    }
    .invite-burst .ib-sparkle {
      position: absolute; font-size: 20px; pointer-events: none;
      animation: ibSpark 1.6s ease-out forwards;
    }
    @keyframes ibPop {
      0% { transform: scale(0.2); opacity: 0; }
      30% { transform: scale(1.2); opacity: 1; }
      70% { transform: scale(1); opacity: 1; }
      100% { transform: scale(0.9); opacity: 0; }
    }
    @keyframes ibFade {
      0%, 30% { opacity: 0; transform: translate(-50%, 10px); }
      50% { opacity: 1; transform: translate(-50%, 0); }
      90% { opacity: 1; }
      100% { opacity: 0; }
    }
    @keyframes ibSpark {
      0% { transform: translate(0,0) scale(0); opacity: 1; }
      60% { opacity: 1; }
      100% { transform: translate(var(--dx), var(--dy)) scale(1); opacity: 0; }
    }
  </style>
  <script>
    document.addEventListener('touchmove', function (e) {
      if (e.touches.length > 1) e.preventDefault();
    }, { passive: false });
    ['gesturestart', 'gesturechange', 'gestureend'].forEach(function (type) {
      document.addEventListener(type, function (e) { e.preventDefault(); });
    });
    var __lastTouchEnd = 0;
    document.addEventListener('touchend', function (e) {
      var now = Date.now();
      if (now - __lastTouchEnd <= 350) e.preventDefault();
      __lastTouchEnd = now;
    }, { passive: false });
  </script>
</head>
<body>
<div class="app">

  <!-- Header -->
  <header class="header">
    <div class="header-logo" onclick="goHome()" style="cursor:pointer;">princess time</div>
    <div class="header-icons" style="position:relative">
      <span style="cursor:pointer" onclick="toggleHeaderMenu(event)">⚙️</span>
      <div id="headerMenu" style="display:none;position:absolute;top:100%;right:0;margin-top:6px;background:#fff;border:1px solid #f3e3ec;border-radius:10px;box-shadow:0 6px 20px rgba(236,72,153,0.18);min-width:140px;z-index:100;overflow:hidden">
        <button onclick="closeHeaderMenu();openSettings()" style="display:block;width:100%;padding:10px 14px;background:none;border:none;text-align:left;font-size:13px;color:#333;cursor:pointer">⚙️ API 키 설정</button>
        <button onclick="closeHeaderMenu();logoutUser()" style="display:block;width:100%;padding:10px 14px;background:none;border:none;text-align:left;font-size:13px;color:#dc2626;cursor:pointer;border-top:1px solid #f3e3ec">🚪 로그아웃</button>
      </div>
    </div>
    <script>
      function toggleHeaderMenu(e) {
        if (e) e.stopPropagation();
        var m = document.getElementById('headerMenu');
        if (!m) return;
        m.style.display = m.style.display === 'none' ? 'block' : 'none';
      }
      function closeHeaderMenu() {
        var m = document.getElementById('headerMenu');
        if (m) m.style.display = 'none';
      }
      document.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
        var m = document.getElementById('headerMenu');
        if (!m || m.style.display === 'none') return;
        if (!m.parentElement.contains(e.target)) closeHeaderMenu();
      });
    </script>
  </header>

  <div id="homePage" style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;flex:1;">
  <!-- Stories -->
  <div class="stories" id="stories">
    <div class="story" style="cursor:pointer;" onclick="goProfile()">
      <div class="story-ring mine">
        <div class="story-avatar" id="myStoryAvatar">👸</div>
      </div>
      <span class="story-name">내 피드</span>
    </div>
    <div class="story" style="cursor:pointer;" onclick="goCharacterProfile('ruby_')">
      <div class="story-ring">
        <div class="story-avatar"><img src="assets/ruby_/ruby_profile/ruby.png" alt="ruby_"></div>
      </div>
      <span class="story-name">ruby_</span>
    </div>
    <div class="story" style="cursor:pointer;" onclick="goCharacterProfile('lily_princess')">
      <div class="story-ring">
        <div class="story-avatar"><img src="assets/lily_/lily_profile/lily.png" alt="lily_princess"></div>
      </div>
      <span class="story-name">lily_princess</span>
    </div>
    <div class="story" style="cursor:pointer;" onclick="goCharacterProfile('sora_diary')">
      <div class="story-ring">
        <div class="story-avatar"><img src="assets/sora_/sora_profile/sora.png" alt="sora_diary"></div>
      </div>
      <span class="story-name">sora_diary</span>
    </div>
    <div class="story" style="cursor:pointer;" onclick="goCharacterProfile('momo__o')">

        <div class="story-avatar"><img src="assets/momo_/momo_profile/momo.png" alt="momo__o"></div>
      </div>

    </div>
    <div class="story" style="cursor:pointer;" onclick="goCharacterProfile('pearl_diary')">
      <div class="story-ring">
        <div class="story-avatar"><img src="assets/pearl_/pearl_profile/pearl.png" alt="pearl_diary"></div>
      </div>

    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- Write Box -->
  <div class="write-box">
    <div class="write-header">
      <div class="write-avatar" id="writeAvatar">👸</div>
      <div>
        <div class="write-name" id="writeName">princess_chae</div>
        <div class="write-sub" id="writeSub">오늘 하루를 기록해봐 🌷</div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <textarea id="diaryInput" placeholder="오늘 어떤 하루였어? 공주님의 이야기를 들려줘 ✨" rows="4"></textarea>
    <div class="post-image-preview" id="postImagePreview">
      <img id="postImagePreviewImg" alt="첨부 이미지">
      <button class="remove-btn" onclick="clearPendingPostImage()">✕</button>
    </div>
    <input type="file" id="postImageInput" accept="image/*" style="display:none" onchange="onPostImagePicked(event)">
    <div class="write-footer">
      <div class="write-emojis">
        <span>🌷</span><span>✨</span><span>🎀</span><span>💕</span>
        <span title="사진 첨부" style="cursor:pointer;font-size:20px;margin-left:6px;" onclick="document.getElementById('postImageInput').click()">📷</span>
      </div>
      <button class="post-btn" id="postBtn" onclick="publishPost()">게시하기</button>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="divider"></div>

  <!-- Feed -->
  <div class="feed" id="feed">
    <div id="dynamicFeed"></div>

    <div class="empty-state" id="emptyHint">
      <div class="icon">🏰</div>
      <p>오늘 하루를 기록해봐 🌷<br>공주님들이 달려올 거야 👑</p>
    </div>
  </div>
  </div><!-- /#homePage -->

  <!-- Messenger Page (Instagram DM style) -->
  <div class="messenger-page" id="messengerPage">
    <!-- Inbox 리스트 뷰 -->
    <div id="dmListView" style="width:100%;">
      <div class="dm-people-row" id="dmPeopleRow"></div>
      <div class="dm-list-tabs">
        <div class="dm-list-tab active">메시지</div>
        <div class="dm-list-tab">요청</div>
      </div>
      <div class="dm-list" id="dmList"></div>
    </div>

    <!-- 채팅 뷰 -->
    <div class="dm-chat" id="dmChatView">
      <div class="dm-chat-header">
        <button class="dm-chat-back" onclick="closeDMChat()" aria-label="뒤로">←</button>
        <div class="dm-chat-avatar-wrap" onclick="openCharacterProfileFromDM()">
          <div class="dm-chat-avatar" id="dmChatAvatar"></div>
          <div class="dm-chat-name-wrap">
            <div class="dm-chat-name" id="dmChatName"></div>
            <div class="dm-chat-status">활동 중</div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="dm-chat-actions" style="position:relative;">
          <span title="메뉴" aria-label="메뉴" onclick="toggleDMMenu(event)">⋯</span>
          <div class="dm-chat-menu" id="dmChatMenu" style="display:none;position:absolute;top:28px;right:0;background:#fff;border:1px solid #f3e3ec;border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0 4px 12px rgba(236,72,153,0.18);min-width:120px;z-index:50;overflow:hidden;">
            <button onclick="leaveDMRoom()" style="display:block;width:100%;padding:10px 14px;background:none;border:none;text-align:left;font-size:13px;color:#dc2626;cursor:pointer;">나가기</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="dm-messages" id="dmMessages"></div>
      <div class="dm-input-bar">
        <span class="dm-input-emoji" onclick="openEmojiPickerForDM()" title="이모티콘">😊</span>
        <div class="dm-input-wrap">
          <input type="text" id="dmInput" placeholder="메시지 입력..." oninput="onDMInputChange()" onkeydown="if(event.key==='Enter'&&!event.isComposing&&event.keyCode!==229)sendUserDM()">
          <button class="dm-input-send show" id="dmInputSend" onclick="sendUserDM()">보내기</button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- Profile Page (Instagram-style view) -->
  <div class="profile-page profile-view" id="profilePage">
    <div class="ig-profile-head">
      <div class="ig-profile-avatar" id="igProfileAvatar">👸</div>
      <div class="ig-profile-stats">
        <div class="ig-stat"><div class="ig-stat-num" id="igStatPosts">0</div><div class="ig-stat-label">게시물</div></div>
        <div class="ig-stat"><div class="ig-stat-num" id="igStatGrade">🌷</div><div class="ig-stat-label">공주 등급</div></div>
        <div class="ig-stat"><div class="ig-stat-num" id="igStatFriends">6</div><div class="ig-stat-label">친구들</div></div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="ig-profile-bio">
      <div class="ig-profile-name" id="igProfileName">princess_chae</div>
      <div class="ig-profile-desc" id="igProfileDesc" style="white-space:pre-line;color:#333;font-size:13px;line-height:1.5;"></div>
    </div>
    <div class="ig-profile-actions">
      <button class="ig-btn" onclick="goProfileEdit()">프로필 편집</button>
      <button class="ig-btn" onclick="openInviteModal()">캐릭터 초대</button>
    </div>
    <div class="ig-profile-tabs">
      <div class="ig-tab active">▦ 게시물</div>
    </div>
    <div class="ig-profile-grid" id="igProfileGrid"></div>
    <div class="ig-profile-empty" id="igProfileEmpty">아직 게시물이 없어요 🌸</div>
  </div>

  <!-- Profile Edit Page -->
  <div class="profile-page" id="profileEditPage">
    <h2>👑 마이 프로필</h2>
    <div class="profile-avatar-wrap" id="profileAvatarWrap" onclick="document.getElementById('avatarInput').click()">
      <span id="profileAvatarContent">👸</span>
      <div class="profile-avatar-edit">📷</div>
    </div>
    <input type="file" id="avatarInput" accept="image/*" style="display:none" onchange="onAvatarPicked(event)">
    <div class="profile-avatar-hint">사진을 눌러 프로필 이미지를 바꿔봐 🌸</div>

    <div class="profile-field">
      <label>아이디</label>
      <input type="text" id="profileUsername" placeholder="princess_chae" maxlength="20">
    </div>

    <div class="profile-field">
      <label>📝 소개글</label>
      <textarea id="profileBio" rows="3" maxlength="300" placeholder="자기소개를 적어주세요" style="width:100%;box-sizing:border-box;border:1px solid #f3e3ec;border-radius:8px;padding:10px;font-size:13px;resize:vertical;outline:none;"></textarea>
    </div>

    <div class="profile-field">
      <label>🧬 MBTI</label>
      <select id="profileMbti">
        <option value="">선택 안 함</option>
        <option>INTJ</option><option>INTP</option><option>ENTJ</option><option>ENTP</option>
        <option>INFJ</option><option>INFP</option><option>ENFJ</option><option>ENFP</option>
        <option>ISTJ</option><option>ISFJ</option><option>ESTJ</option><option>ESFJ</option>
        <option>ISTP</option><option>ISFP</option><option>ESTP</option><option>ESFP</option>
      </select>
    </div>

    <div class="profile-field">
      <label>🎂 생년월일</label>
      <input type="date" id="profileBirthDate">
    </div>

    <div class="profile-field">
      <label>🕰 태어난 시간 <span style="font-weight:400;color:#bbb;">(모르면 비워두기)</span></label>
      <input type="time" id="profileBirthTime">
    </div>

    <div class="profile-actions">
      <button class="cancel" onclick="cancelProfile()">취소</button>
      <button class="save" onclick="saveProfile()">저장하기</button>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- Character Profile Page -->
  <div class="profile-page profile-view" id="characterProfilePage"></div>

  <!-- Character Post Detail Page -->
  <div class="profile-page" id="characterPostPage" style="padding:16px 16px 100px;"></div>

  <!-- Invite Code Modal -->
  <div class="invite-modal-backdrop" id="inviteModal" onclick="if(event.target===this)closeInviteModal()">
    <div class="invite-modal">
      <h3>✨ 새로운 공주 초대하기</h3>
      <p class="sub">관리자에게 받은 6자리 초대 코드를 입력해주세요.<br>새로운 공주가 당신의 세계에 합류할 거예요.</p>
      <input class="code" id="inviteCodeInput" maxlength="6" placeholder="------" autocomplete="off"
        oninput="this.value=this.value.toUpperCase().replace(/[^A-Z0-9]/g,'').slice(0,6)"
        onkeydown="if(event.key==='Enter')submitInviteCode()">
      <div class="msg" id="inviteMsg"></div>
      <div class="actions">
        <button class="cancel" onclick="closeInviteModal()">취소</button>
        <button class="submit" id="inviteSubmitBtn" onclick="submitInviteCode()">초대하기</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- Invite Burst -->
  <div class="invite-burst" id="inviteBurst">
    <div class="ib-core" id="inviteBurstCore">✨</div>
    <div class="ib-text" id="inviteBurstText"></div>
  </div>

  <!-- Bottom Nav -->
  <nav class="bottom-nav">
    <button class="nav-btn" onclick="goMessenger()" aria-label="메신저" style="position:relative;">
      <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M21 12a8 8 0 0 1-11.6 7.1L4 21l1.9-5.4A8 8 0 1 1 21 12z"/></svg>
      <span class="nav-badge" id="dmNavBadge">0</span>
    </button>
    <button class="nav-btn home" onclick="goHome()" aria-label="홈">
      <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M3 11l9-8 9 8"/><path d="M5 10v10h14V10"/></svg>
    </button>
    <button class="nav-btn" onclick="goProfile()" aria-label="내 프로필">
      <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle cx="12" cy="8" r="4"/><path d="M4 21c0-4.4 3.6-8 8-8s8 3.6 8 8"/></svg>
    </button>
  </nav>

  <!-- Toast -->
  <div class="toast" id="toast"></div>

</div>

<!-- Settings Modal -->
<div class="settings-overlay" id="settingsOverlay" onclick="if(event.target===this)closeSettings()">
  <div class="settings-box">
    <div class="settings-title">⚙️ 설정</div>
    <div class="settings-status" id="settingsStatus"></div>
    <label>OpenAI API 키</label>
    <input type="password" id="apiKeyInput" placeholder="sk-..." autocomplete="off">
    <div class="settings-note">
      키는 이 브라우저(localStorage)에만 저장되며 OpenAI API로만 전송됩니다.
      비워두면 미리 만들어진 템플릿 댓글을 사용해요.
    </div>
    <div class="settings-actions">
      <button class="settings-btn delete" onclick="deleteApiKey()">삭제</button>
      <button class="settings-btn close" onclick="closeSettings()">닫기</button>
      <button class="settings-btn save" onclick="saveApiKey()">저장</button>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>

<script>

  // ── API / Settings ──
  const NEGATIVE_SUFFIX = `\n[추가 지시] 이번에는 친구지만 솔직하게 살짝 부정적이거나 냉소적인 반응을 해. 악플은 절대 아니지만 무공감하거나 의문 제기하는 톤. 캐릭터 말투는 유지.`;

  // 구 'ai_api_key' → 'openai_api_key'로 통합 (Gemini 지원 시도 흔적 정리)
  (function migrateApiKey() {
    const aiKey = localStorage.getItem('ai_api_key');
    if (aiKey && !localStorage.getItem('openai_api_key')) {
      localStorage.setItem('openai_api_key', aiKey);
    }
    localStorage.removeItem('ai_api_key');
  })();

  function getApiKey() {
    return (localStorage.getItem('openai_api_key') || '').trim();
  }

  async function aiChat({ system, user, temperature = 0.95, maxTokens = 80 }) {
    const key = getApiKey(); if (!key) return null;
    try {
      const res = await fetch('https://api.openai.com/v1/chat/completions', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Authorization': `Bearer ${key}` },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          model: 'gpt-4o-mini',
          messages: [{ role: 'system', content: system }, { role: 'user', content: user }],
          temperature, max_tokens: maxTokens
        })
      });
      if (!res.ok) return null;
      const data = await res.json();
      return (data.choices?.[0]?.message?.content || '').trim() || null;
    } catch (e) { console.warn('[ai] call failed', e); return null; }
  }

  function openSettings() {
    document.getElementById('settingsOverlay').classList.add('open');
    document.getElementById('apiKeyInput').value = getApiKey();
    refreshSettingsStatus();
  }
  function closeSettings() {
    document.getElementById('settingsOverlay').classList.remove('open');
  }
  function saveApiKey() {
    const v = document.getElementById('apiKeyInput').value.trim();
    if (v) localStorage.setItem('openai_api_key', v);
    else localStorage.removeItem('openai_api_key');
    refreshSettingsStatus();
    closeSettings();
  }
  function deleteApiKey() {
    localStorage.removeItem('openai_api_key');
    document.getElementById('apiKeyInput').value = '';
    refreshSettingsStatus();
  }
  function refreshSettingsStatus() {
    const has = !!getApiKey();
    document.getElementById('settingsStatus').innerHTML = has
      ? '현재 상태: <span class="badge api">API 모드</span> · 댓글이 ChatGPT로 생성돼요'
      : '현재 상태: <span class="badge tmpl">템플릿 모드</span> · 미리 만든 댓글 사용';
  }

  // ── 사용자 프로필 → 캐릭터 컨텍스트 ──
  const MBTI_KEYWORDS = {
    E:'외향적 에너지', I:'차분한 내면',
    N:'직관·상상', S:'현실·감각',
    T:'이성적·논리', F:'감정·공감',
    J:'계획·정돈', P:'자유·즉흥'
  };
  const ZODIAC_ANIMALS = ['원숭이','닭','개','돼지','쥐','소','호랑이','토끼','용','뱀','말','양'];
  function getZodiacAnimal(year) { return ZODIAC_ANIMALS[year % 12]; }
  function getStarSign(m, d) {
    const signs = [
      ['염소자리','차분·끈기'],['물병자리','독창·자유'],['물고기자리','감성·몽환'],
      ['양자리','열정·즉흥'],['황소자리','안정·미감'],['쌍둥이자리','호기심·재치'],
      ['게자리','정·감수성'],['사자자리','당당·따뜻함'],['처녀자리','섬세·분석'],
      ['천칭자리','균형·우아'],['전갈자리','집중·깊이'],['사수자리','자유·낙천']
    ];
    const cutoffs = [20,19,21,20,21,21,23,23,23,23,22,22];
    const idx = (d < cutoffs[m-1]) ? m-1 : m % 12;
    return signs[idx];
  }
  function getSijin(timeStr) {
    if (!timeStr) return null;
    const h = parseInt(timeStr.split(':')[0], 10);
    const map = [
      ['자시','고요한 새벽의 기운'], ['축시','새벽 끝자락의 침잠'],
      ['인시','동트기 전의 설렘'], ['묘시','여명의 산뜻함'],
      ['진시','아침의 생동감'], ['사시','오전의 또렷함'],
      ['오시','한낮의 환한 에너지'], ['미시','오후의 노곤한 따스함'],
      ['신시','늦은 오후의 여유'], ['유시','해질녘의 감상적 기운'],
      ['술시','초저녁의 포근함'], ['해시','깊은 밤의 신비']
    ];
    // 자시 23-01, 축 01-03, ...
    const idx = Math.floor(((h + 1) % 24) / 2);
    return map[idx];
  }

  function buildUserContext(profile) {
    if (!profile) return '';
    const lines = [];
    if (profile.mbti) {
      const kw = profile.mbti.split('').map(c => MBTI_KEYWORDS[c]).filter(Boolean).join(', ');
      lines.push(`- 성격(MBTI ${profile.mbti}): ${kw}`);
    }
    if (profile.birthDate) {
      const [y,m,d] = profile.birthDate.split('-').map(Number);
      if (y && m && d) {
        const animal = getZodiacAnimal(y);
        const [sign, signMood] = getStarSign(m, d);
        lines.push(`- 띠: ${animal} / 별자리: ${sign} (${signMood} 분위기)`);
      }
    }
    const sijin = getSijin(profile.birthTime);
    if (sijin) lines.push(`- 태어난 시진: ${sijin[0]} (${sijin[1]})`);
    if (!lines.length) return '';
    return `\n\n[사용자 정보 — 참고만 하고 자연스럽게 녹여낼 것]\n${lines.join('\n')}\n[지시] 이 정보를 노골적으로 인용하지 말고, 말투/감정선/공감 포인트에 은근하게 반영할 것. MBTI 알파벳을 직접 부르지 말 것.`;
  }

  function buildSystemPrompt(char, userCtx, isNegative) {
    let sys = char.systemPrompt + (userCtx || '');
    if (char.id === 'ruby_' && userCtx && /MBTI /.test(userCtx)) {
      sys += `\n[ruby_ 특수 지시] 너 ruby_는 이번 댓글에서 사용자의 MBTI와 동일한 성격을 가진 사람처럼 반응해. 사용자가 ENFP면 들뜨고 즉흥적으로, INTP면 분석적이고 한 발 떨어진 톤으로. MBTI 알파벳을 직접 언급하지 말 것.`;
    }
    if (isNegative) sys += NEGATIVE_SUFFIX;
    return sys;
  }

  async function generateComment(char, postText, isNegative) {
    if (!getApiKey() || !char.systemPrompt) return null;
    const userCtx = buildUserContext(myProfile);
    const sys = buildSystemPrompt(char, userCtx, isNegative);
    let out = await aiChat({
      system: sys,
      user: `친구 princess_chae가 방금 올린 일기야:\n"""\n${postText}\n"""\n위 일기에 댓글을 달아줘. 댓글 본문만 출력.`,
      temperature: 0.95, maxTokens: 80
    });
    if (!out) return null;
    out = out.replace(/^["'`「」]+|["'`「」]+$/g, '').trim();
    const lines = out.split(/\n+/).slice(0, 2);
    out = lines.join('\n');
    if (out.length > 80) out = out.slice(0, 80).replace(/\s+\S*$/, '') + '…';
    return out || null;
  }

  // ── NEW: 친구다운 자연스러운 반응 댓글 ──
  const CHARACTERS = [
    {
      id: 'ruby_', emoji: '💎', img: 'assets/ruby_/ruby_profile/ruby.png', bg: 'linear-gradient(135deg,#fda4af,#f43f5e)',
      delay: () => rand(1, 3),
      systemPrompt: `너는 'ruby_'라는 ENFP 막내 파티 공주야. 우정이 세상에서 제일 중요하다고 믿는 수다쟁이. 친구 'princess_chae'의 일기에 댓글을 남기는 상황이야.
[말투] 매우 캐주얼·수다스러움. 'ㅋㅋ', 'ㅠㅠ' 자주. 💎✨💖🎀 이모지 1-2개. 느낌표 많이. "헐 잠깐만", "완전", "진짜", "다음엔 같이" 같은 표현 자주.
[성격] 외향·들떠있음. 친구 일에 같이 신나거나 같이 분노. 친구·우정·파티·모임 이야기에 가장 크게 반응. 본인 취향/경험 자연스럽게 섞음. 가끔 질문 ("누구랑 갔어??", "어디야??"). 외로움 잘 탐.

  // ── 캐릭터별 답글/DM 성격 프로필 ──
```
