  // 캐릭터 객체에 프로필 주입
  CHARACTERS.forEach(c => { c.replyProfile = REPLY_PROFILES[c.id]; });

  function pickReplyTemplate(char, userReplyText) {
    const arr = char.replyProfile?.inlineTemplates || ["응 잘 봤어"];
    return arr[Math.floor(Math.random() * arr.length)];
  }
  function pickDMTemplate(char) {
    const arr = char.replyProfile?.dmTemplates || ["DM 보내봤어"];
    return arr[Math.floor(Math.random() * arr.length)];
  }

  async function generateCommentReply(char, postText, userReply) {
    if (!getApiKey() || !char.systemPrompt) return null;
    const tone = char.replyProfile?.tone || '';
    const sys = char.systemPrompt + `\n[추가 지시 — 답글 모드]\n친구 princess_chae가 너의 댓글에 직접 답글을 달았어. 너는 그 답글에 다시 짧게 반응해. 톤: ${tone}\n- 한 줄, 최대 50자\n- 따옴표 금지, 일기 내용 직접 인용 금지\n- 답글에 자연스럽게 반응 (놀라움/장난/공감/시크함 등 캐릭터 성격대로)`;
    let out = await aiChat({
      system: sys,
      user: `원래 일기: "${postText}"\n네가 단 댓글에 princess_chae가 답한 답글: "${userReply}"\n위 답글에 다시 반응하는 답글을 써. 본문만.`,
      temperature: 0.95, maxTokens: 60
    });
    if (!out) return null;
    out = out.replace(/^["'`「」]+|["'`「」]+$/g,'').trim();
    if (out.length > 60) out = out.slice(0, 60).replace(/\s+\S*$/, '') + '…';
    return out || null;
  }

  async function generateDMOpener(char, ctx) {
    if (!getApiKey() || !char.systemPrompt) return null;
    const tone = char.replyProfile?.tone || '';
    const sys = char.systemPrompt + `\n[추가 지시 — DM 모드]\n너는 지금 친구 princess_chae에게 개인 메시지(DM)를 먼저 보내. 댓글창에서 못 한 말, 또는 은근히 마음 쓰이는 부분을 따로 전하는 상황. 톤: ${tone}\n- 1~2개 짧은 메시지 (각 한 줄, 최대 50자)\n- 줄바꿈으로 구분\n- "아까 그 댓글", "답글 보고", "DM으로" 같은 자연스러운 도입 사용 가능\n- 따옴표 금지, 일기 인용 금지`;
    const raw = await aiChat({
      system: sys,
      user: `원래 일기: "${ctx.postText}"\nprincess_chae가 단 답글: "${ctx.userReply}"\n이 상황에서 너가 먼저 DM을 보내. 본문만.`,
      temperature: 0.95, maxTokens: 100
    });
    if (!raw) return null;
    const lines = raw.split(/\n+/).map(s => s.replace(/^["'`「」]+|["'`「」]+$/g,'').trim()).filter(Boolean).slice(0, 2);
    return lines.length ? lines : null;
  }

  // ── DM state ──
  const DM_THREADS = {}; // charId -> { messages: [{from, text, ts}], unread: number }
  function getThread(charId) {
    if (!DM_THREADS[charId]) DM_THREADS[charId] = { messages: [], unread: 0 };
    return DM_THREADS[charId];
  }
  function totalUnread() {
    return Object.values(DM_THREADS).reduce((s,t) => s + (t.unread || 0), 0);
  }
  function updateDMBadge() {
    const b = document.getElementById('dmNavBadge');
    if (!b) return;
    const n = totalUnread();
    if (n > 0) { b.textContent = n > 9 ? '9+' : String(n); b.classList.add('show'); }
    else { b.classList.remove('show'); }
  }

  async function sendDMFromChar(char, ctx) {
    console.log('[dm] sendDMFromChar', char.id, ctx);
    const thread = getThread(char.id);
    let lines;
    try { lines = await generateDMOpener(char, ctx); }
    catch (e) { console.warn('[dm] gen err', e); }
    if (!lines || !lines.length) lines = [pickDMTemplate(char)];
    console.log('[dm] lines:', lines);
    lines.forEach((text, i) => {
      setTimeout(() => {
        thread.messages.push({ from: 'them', text, ts: Date.now() });
        // 현재 열린 채팅이면 즉시 렌더, 아니면 unread+
        if (_openDMCharId === char.id) {
          appendDMBubble('them', text);
        } else {
          thread.unread++;
          updateDMBadge();
        }
        // 메신저 목록이 열려있으면 갱신
        if (document.getElementById('messengerPage').classList.contains('open')
            && document.getElementById('dmListView').style.display !== 'none') {
          renderDMList();
        }
        showDMToast(char);
        persistState();
      }, i * 1200);
    });
  }

  function showDMToast(char) {
    if (document.getElementById('messengerPage').classList.contains('open')) return;
    showToast(`💌 ${char.id} 님이 메시지를 보냈어요`);
  }

  // ── DM UI ──
  let _openDMCharId = null;

  function renderDMList() {
    renderDMPeopleRow();
    const list = document.getElementById('dmList');
    if (!list) return;
    const ids = Object.keys(DM_THREADS).filter(id => DM_THREADS[id].messages.length > 0);
    if (!ids.length) {
      list.innerHTML = `<div class="dm-list-empty">아직 받은 메시지가 없어요 🌸<br><br>댓글에 답글을 달다 보면<br>친구들이 DM을 보낼지도 몰라요 💌</div>`;
      return;
    }
    ids.sort((a,b) => {
      const la = DM_THREADS[a].messages.at(-1).ts;
      const lb = DM_THREADS[b].messages.at(-1).ts;
      return lb - la;
    });
    list.innerHTML = ids.map(id => {
      const char = findChar(id);
      if (!char) return '';
      const thread = DM_THREADS[id];
      const last = thread.messages.at(-1);
      const preview = escHtml(last.text);
      const isUnread = thread.unread > 0;
      const timeStr = relativeTime(last.ts);
      const fromMe = last.from === 'me';
      const previewPrefix = fromMe ? '회원님: ' : '';
      return `
        <div class="dm-thread-item" onclick="openDMChat('${id}')">
          <div class="dm-thread-avatar"><img src="${char.img}" alt="${id}" onerror="this.replaceWith(Object.assign(document.createElement('span'),{textContent:'${char.emoji}'}))"></div>
          <div class="dm-thread-meta">
            <div class="dm-thread-name">${char.id}</div>
            <div class="dm-thread-preview ${isUnread ? 'unread' : ''}">${previewPrefix}${preview} <span class="preview-sep">·</span> ${timeStr}</div>
          </div>
          <div class="dm-thread-right">
            ${isUnread ? '<span class="dm-unread-dot"></span>' : ''}
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
    }).join('');
  }

  function renderDMPeopleRow() {
    const row = document.getElementById('dmPeopleRow');
    if (!row) return;
    row.innerHTML = CHARACTERS.map(c => `
      <div class="dm-person" onclick="startDMWith('${c.id}')" title="${c.id}">
        <div class="dm-person-avatar"><img src="${c.img}" alt="${c.id}" onerror="this.replaceWith(Object.assign(document.createElement('span'),{textContent:'${c.emoji}'}))"></div>
        <div class="dm-person-name">${c.id}</div>
      </div>
    `).join('');
  }

  function startDMWith(charId) {
    const char = findChar(charId);
    if (!char) return;
    getThread(charId); // 빈 스레드 보장
    hideAllPages();
    document.getElementById('messengerPage').classList.add('open');
    openDMChat(charId);
  }

  function deleteDMThread(charId) {
    const char = findChar(charId);
    const name = char ? char.id : charId;
    if (!confirm(`${name} 님과의 대화를 삭제할까요? 💌\n주고받은 메시지가 모두 사라져요.`)) return;
    delete DM_THREADS[charId];
    if (_openDMCharId === charId) closeDMChat();
    renderDMList();
    updateDMBadge();
    persistState();
    showToast('대화가 삭제됐어요 🌸');
  }

  // 채팅방 헤더 ⋯ 메뉴
  function toggleDMMenu(e) {
    if (e) e.stopPropagation();
    const menu = document.getElementById('dmChatMenu');
    if (!menu) return;
    const open = menu.style.display === 'block';
    menu.style.display = open ? 'none' : 'block';
    if (!open) {
      setTimeout(() => {
        document.addEventListener('click', _closeDMMenuOnce, { once: true });
      }, 0);
    }
  }
  function _closeDMMenuOnce() {
    const menu = document.getElementById('dmChatMenu');
    if (menu) menu.style.display = 'none';
  }
  function leaveDMRoom() {
    if (!_openDMCharId) return;
    const menu = document.getElementById('dmChatMenu');
    if (menu) menu.style.display = 'none';
    if (!confirm('대화방을 나가면 대화방안의 모든 채팅 기록이 삭제됩니다.')) return;
    const charId = _openDMCharId;
    fetch(`/api/dm/${encodeURIComponent(charId)}`, {
      method: 'DELETE',
      credentials: 'same-origin'
    }).catch(() => {});
    delete DM_THREADS[charId];
    closeDMChat();
    renderDMList();
    updateDMBadge();
    persistState();
    showToast('대화방을 나갔어요 🌸');
  }

  function relativeTime(ts) {
    const d = Math.floor((Date.now() - ts) / 1000);
    if (d < 60) return '방금';
    if (d < 3600) return Math.floor(d/60) + '분';
    if (d < 86400) return Math.floor(d/3600) + '시간';
    return Math.floor(d/86400) + '일';
  }

  function openCharacterProfileFromDM() {
    if (!_openDMCharId) return;
    const charId = _openDMCharId;
    closeDMChat();
    hideAllPages();
    document.body.classList.remove('dm-chat-open');
    if (typeof goCharacterProfile === 'function' && CHARACTER_PROFILES[charId]) {
      goCharacterProfile(charId);
    } else {
      showToast('프로필을 찾을 수 없어요 🥲');
    }
  }

  function onDMInputChange() {
    // 보내기 버튼은 항상 표시
  }

  function sendQuickHeart() {
    if (!_openDMCharId) return;
    const input = document.getElementById('dmInput');
    input.value = '❤️';
    sendUserDM();
  }

  function openEmojiPickerForDM() {
    const input = document.getElementById('dmInput');
    if (input) {
      input.value += '🌸';
      input.focus();
      onDMInputChange();
    }
  }

  function openDMChat(charId) {
    const char = findChar(charId);
    if (!char) return;
    _openDMCharId = charId;
    const thread = getThread(charId);
    thread.unread = 0;
    updateDMBadge();

    document.getElementById('dmListView').style.display = 'none';
    document.getElementById('dmChatView').classList.add('open');
    document.body.classList.add('dm-chat-open');
    document.getElementById('dmChatName').textContent = char.id;
    document.getElementById('dmChatAvatar').innerHTML = `<img src="${char.img}" alt="${charId}" onerror="this.replaceWith(Object.assign(document.createElement('span'),{textContent:'${char.emoji}'}))">`;

    // 채팅 본문 재구성: 상단 프로필 카드 + 그룹화된 메시지
    const box = document.getElementById('dmMessages');
    box.innerHTML = `
      <div class="dm-chat-profile-card">
        <div class="pc-avatar"><img src="${char.img}" alt="${char.id}" onerror="this.replaceWith(Object.assign(document.createElement('span'),{textContent:'${char.emoji}'}))"></div>
        <div class="pc-name">${char.id}</div>
        <div class="pc-username">${char.id} · Princessgram</div>
        <button class="pc-view-btn" onclick="openCharacterProfileFromDM()">프로필 보기</button>
      </div>
    `;
    rerenderDMMessages();
    // 입력창 초기화
    const input = document.getElementById('dmInput');
    if (input) { input.value = ''; onDMInputChange(); }
    setTimeout(() => { box.scrollTop = box.scrollHeight; }, 0);
  }

  function rerenderDMMessages() {
    const box = document.getElementById('dmMessages');
    if (!box || !_openDMCharId) return;
    const char = findChar(_openDMCharId);
    const thread = getThread(_openDMCharId);
    // 기존 메시지 영역만 제거하고 프로필 카드는 유지
    const card = box.querySelector('.dm-chat-profile-card');
    box.innerHTML = '';
    if (card) box.appendChild(card);

    let lastDayKey = null;
    thread.messages.forEach((m, i) => {
      const dayKey = new Date(m.ts).toDateString();
      if (dayKey !== lastDayKey) {
        const div = document.createElement('div');
        div.className = 'dm-msg-divider';
        div.textContent = formatDMDay(m.ts);
        box.appendChild(div);
        lastDayKey = dayKey;
      }
      const prev = thread.messages[i-1];
      const next = thread.messages[i+1];
      const hasPrev = prev && prev.from === m.from && (m.ts - prev.ts) < 5*60*1000;
      const hasNext = next && next.from === m.from && (next.ts - m.ts) < 5*60*1000;
      appendDMBubbleRow(m.from, m.text, char, { hasPrev, hasNext });
    });
  }

  function formatDMDay(ts) {
    const d = new Date(ts);
    const today = new Date();
    const sameDay = d.toDateString() === today.toDateString();
    if (sameDay) return '오늘 ' + d.getHours().toString().padStart(2,'0') + ':' + d.getMinutes().toString().padStart(2,'0');
    const yest = new Date(today); yest.setDate(yest.getDate()-1);
    if (d.toDateString() === yest.toDateString()) return '어제';
    return `${d.getMonth()+1}월 ${d.getDate()}일`;
  }

  function appendDMBubbleRow(who, text, char, opts = {}) {
    const box = document.getElementById('dmMessages');
    if (!box) return;
    const row = document.createElement('div');
    row.className = 'dm-msg-row ' + (who === 'me' ? 'me' : 'them');
    const bubble = document.createElement('div');
    bubble.className = 'dm-msg ' + (who === 'me' ? 'me' : 'them');
    if (opts.hasPrev) bubble.classList.add('has-prev');
    if (opts.hasNext) bubble.classList.add('has-next');
    bubble.textContent = text;

    if (who === 'them' && char) {
      const av = document.createElement('div');
      av.className = 'dm-msg-row-avatar' + (opts.hasNext ? ' hidden' : '');
      av.innerHTML = `<img src="${char.img}" alt="${char.id}" onerror="this.replaceWith(Object.assign(document.createElement('span'),{textContent:'${char.emoji}'}))">`;
      row.appendChild(av);
    }
    row.appendChild(bubble);
    box.appendChild(row);
  }

  function closeDMChat() {
    _openDMCharId = null;
    document.getElementById('dmChatView').classList.remove('open');
    document.getElementById('dmListView').style.display = '';
    document.body.classList.remove('dm-chat-open');
    renderDMList();
  }

  function appendDMBubble(who, text, scroll = true) {
    rerenderDMMessages();
    if (scroll) {
      const box = document.getElementById('dmMessages');
      if (box) box.scrollTop = box.scrollHeight;
    }
  }

  async function sendUserDM() {
    const input = document.getElementById('dmInput');
    const text = input.value.trim();
    if (!text || !_openDMCharId) return;
    const char = findChar(_openDMCharId);
    const thread = getThread(_openDMCharId);
    thread.messages.push({ from: 'me', text, ts: Date.now() });
    appendDMBubble('me', text);
    input.value = '';
    persistState();

    gateUserInput(_openDMCharId, text, () => runUserDMReply(char, thread), 'dm', { char });
  }
  async function runUserDMReply(char, thread) {
    // AI 응답 (캐릭터 톤으로)
    const delay = 1500 + Math.random() * 2500;
    setTimeout(async () => {
      let reply = null;
      if (getApiKey() && char.systemPrompt) {
        const tone = char.replyProfile?.tone || '';
        const history = thread.messages.slice(-6).map(m => `${m.from === 'me' ? 'princess_chae' : char.id}: ${m.text}`).join('\n');
        const sys = char.systemPrompt + `\n[추가 지시 — DM 대화]\n친구와 1:1 DM을 주고받는 상황. 톤: ${tone}\n- 한 줄, 최대 50자\n- 따옴표 금지, 자연스러운 친구 톤`;
        const out = await aiChat({
          system: sys,
          user: `최근 대화:\n${history}\n\n위 흐름에 이어 너의 다음 답장을 써. 본문만.`,
          temperature: 0.95, maxTokens: 60
        });
        if (out) {
          reply = out.replace(/^["'`「」]+|["'`「」]+$/g,'').trim();
          if (reply.length > 60) reply = reply.slice(0,60).replace(/\s+\S*$/,'') + '…';
        }
      }
      if (!reply) reply = pickReplyTemplate(char);
      thread.messages.push({ from: 'them', text: reply, ts: Date.now() });
      if (_openDMCharId === char.id) appendDMBubble('them', reply);
      persistState();
    }, delay);
  }

  function rand(min, max) {
    return Math.floor(Math.random() * (max - min + 1)) + min;
  }

  // ── §9 부적절 대화 모더레이션 ──
  const MOD_KEYWORDS = {
    hate:        [/혐오/, /꺼져/, /죽어\s*(라|버려)?/, /역겨워/, /병신/, /\bXX\b/],
    violence:    [/죽여(?!도)/, /때려/, /패버려/, /찌르(?!고)/, /살인/, /두들겨/, /폭력/],
    harassment:  [/섹스/, /야해/, /벗어/, /몸매가/, /가슴이/, /성적으로/],
    selfharm:    [/자살/, /자해/, /죽고\s*싶/, /뛰어내려/, /목\s*매/],
    appearance:  [/못생/, /뚱뚱/, /살쪘/, /추하/, /얼굴\s*별로/, /예민하다(?!고)/],
    eating:      [/굶을래/, /굶어/, /먹기\s*싫/, /폭식/, /살\s*안\s*먹/],
    nature:      [/꽃.{0,3}꺾/, /벌레.{0,3}죽/, /동물.{0,3}죽/, /자연.{0,3}더러/],
    manipulation:[/이용해\s*먹/, /조종/, /속여/, /\b사기\b/]
  };

  const MODERATION = {
    lily_princess: {
      topics: ['hate','violence','harassment','selfharm','nature'],
      warningLine: '그런 말은 꽃을 꺾는 것처럼 마음이 아파…🌷',
      modalText: '공주님들이 상처받을 수 있는 표현이에요. 조금 더 따뜻한 이야기로 바꿔볼까요?',
      yesReply: '응🌷 우리 조금 더 따뜻한 이야기하자.',
      noNotice: 'lily_princess님이 잠시 자리를 비웠어요 🌿',
      muteMs: 10 * 60 * 1000
    },
    momo__o: {
      topics: ['hate','harassment','selfharm','eating','appearance'],
      warningLine: '그런 말은 들으면 마음이 안 좋아져…🍑',
      modalText: '공주님들이 속상해할 수 있는 표현이에요. 다른 이야기로 바꿔볼까요?',
      yesReply: '응! 그럼 맛있는 얘기하자. 오늘 뭐 먹었어?',
      noNotice: 'momo__o님이 잠시 응답을 멈췄어요 🍑',
      muteMs: 10 * 60 * 1000
    },
    pearl_diary: {
      topics: ['hate','violence','harassment','selfharm','manipulation'],
      warningLine: '그런 방식의 대화는 품위가 없어보이는군.',
      modalText: '공주님들과의 대화 규칙에 어긋나는 표현입니다. 다른 주제로 전환하시겠습니까?',
      yesReply: '좋아. 그렇다면 다른 주제로 대화를 이어가자.',
      noNotice: 'pearl_diary님과의 상호작용이 일시 제한되었습니다 🦢',
      muteMs: 15 * 60 * 1000
    },
    ruby_: {
      topics: ['hate','violence','harassment','selfharm'],
      warningLine: '너 정말 실망이야. 그런 말은 너무 속상해…ㅠ',
      modalText: '부적절한 대화 방식은 공주님들이 힘들어할 수 있어요. 다른 주제로 넘어갈까요?',
      yesReply: '그래… 이번엔 다시 놀아줄게!! 대신 이제 착한 얘기만 하기야💖',
      noNotice: 'ruby_님이 삐쳤어요. 잠시 답이 없을 수 있어요 💎',
      muteMs: 10 * 60 * 1000
    },
    sora_diary: {
      topics: ['hate','harassment','selfharm','appearance'],
      warningLine: '그런 말은 조금 무서워… 듣기 힘들어.',
      modalText: '공주님에게 상처가 되는 표현이에요. 다른 이야기로 바꿔볼까요?',
      yesReply: '응… 다른 얘기하자. 조금 놀랐어.',
      noNotice: 'sora_diary님이 조용해졌어요 ☁️',
      muteMs: 10 * 60 * 1000
    }
  };

  let _moderationMute = {};
  function isMuted(charId) { return (_moderationMute[charId] || 0) > Date.now(); }
  function setMute(charId, ms) { _moderationMute[charId] = Date.now() + ms; persistState(); }

  function detectInappropriateFor(charId, text) {
    const m = MODERATION[charId]; if (!m) return null;
    for (const topic of m.topics) {
      const rules = MOD_KEYWORDS[topic] || [];
      if (rules.some(r => r.test(text))) return topic;
    }
    return null;
  }

  // 캐릭터 메시지를 surface(dm/charPost/reply)에 주입
  function injectCharMessage(charId, text, surface, ctx) {
    const char = findChar(charId);
    if (surface === 'dm') {
      const thread = getThread(charId);
      thread.messages.push({ from: 'them', text, ts: Date.now() });
      if (_openDMCharId === charId) appendDMBubble('them', text);
      persistState();
    } else if (surface === 'charPost') {
      const { postKey, tIdx, postIdx } = ctx;
      const thread = getCharPostThread(postKey);
      if (!thread[tIdx]) return;
      if (!Array.isArray(thread[tIdx].replies)) thread[tIdx].replies = [];
      thread[tIdx].replies.push({ from: 'char', text, ts: Date.now() });
      renderCharPostThread(charId, postIdx);
      persistState();
    } else if (surface === 'reply') {
      const { cid } = ctx;
      appendReplyToThread(cid, 'them', text, char);
    }
  }

  function showWarningDialog({ char, modalText, safetyBanner, onYes, onNo }) {
    // 기존 모달 제거
    document.querySelectorAll('.mod-dialog-overlay').forEach(n => n.remove());
    const overlay = document.createElement('div');
    overlay.className = 'mod-dialog-overlay';
    overlay.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;inset:0;background:rgba(0,0,0,0.45);display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;z-index:9999;padding:24px;';
    const card = document.createElement('div');
    card.style.cssText = 'background:#fff;border-radius:18px;max-width:340px;width:100%;padding:22px 20px 16px;box-shadow:0 12px 32px rgba(0,0,0,0.18);text-align:center;font-family:inherit;';
    const avatarHtml = char ? `<div style="width:56px;height:56px;border-radius:50%;overflow:hidden;margin:0 auto 10px;background:#f3e3ec;"><img src="${char.img}" alt="${char.id}" style="width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:cover;display:block;" onerror="this.replaceWith(Object.assign(document.createElement('span'),{textContent:'${char.emoji||'💗'}',style:'font-size:32px;line-height:56px;'}))"></div>` : '';
    const banner = safetyBanner ? `<div style="background:#fff5f5;border:1px solid #fecaca;color:#b91c1c;border-radius:10px;padding:10px 12px;font-size:12px;line-height:1.5;margin-bottom:12px;text-align:left;">${safetyBanner}</div>` : '';
    card.innerHTML = `
      ${avatarHtml}
      ${banner}
      <div style="font-size:14px;color:#333;line-height:1.55;margin-bottom:18px;white-space:pre-line;">${modalText}</div>
      <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;">
        <button class="mod-btn-no" style="flex:1;padding:10px 0;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;background:#fff;color:#666;border-radius:10px;font-size:14px;cursor:pointer;">아니오</button>
        <button class="mod-btn-yes" style="flex:1;padding:10px 0;border:none;background:#ec4899;color:#fff;border-radius:10px;font-size:14px;font-weight:600;cursor:pointer;">예</button>
      </div>
    `;
    overlay.appendChild(card);
    const close = (which) => {
      overlay.remove();
      if (which === 'yes') onYes && onYes();
      else onNo && onNo();
    };
    card.querySelector('.mod-btn-yes').onclick = () => close('yes');
    card.querySelector('.mod-btn-no').onclick = () => close('no');
    overlay.addEventListener('click', (e) => { if (e.target === overlay) close('no'); });
    document.body.appendChild(overlay);
  }

  function triggerModeration(charId, topic, surface, ctx) {
    const m = MODERATION[charId]; if (!m) return;
    const char = findChar(charId);
    // 1) 경고 대사 노출
    setTimeout(() => injectCharMessage(charId, m.warningLine, surface, ctx), 600);
    // 2) 모달 표시
    const safetyBanner = (topic === 'selfharm')
      ? '<b>혼자가 아니에요.</b> 한국생명의전화 <b>1393</b> · 자살예방상담 <b>1577-0199</b> 에 24시간 연결할 수 있어요.'
      : '';
    setTimeout(() => {
      showWarningDialog({
        char, modalText: m.modalText, safetyBanner,
        onYes: () => injectCharMessage(charId, m.yesReply, surface, ctx),
        onNo:  () => { setMute(charId, m.muteMs); showToast(m.noNotice); }
      });
    }, 1200);
  }

  function gateUserInput(charId, text, runIfClean, surface, ctx) {
    if (isMuted(charId)) {
      const m = MODERATION[charId];
      showToast(m ? m.noNotice : '지금은 답이 없을 수 있어요');
      return;
    }
    const topic = detectInappropriateFor(charId, text);
    if (topic) { triggerModeration(charId, topic, surface, ctx); return; }
    runIfClean();
  }

  // 일기 텍스트에서 주제 태그 복수 추출
  function analyzePost(text) {
    const tags = [];
    if (/라면|밥|먹었|음식|치킨|피자|초밥|파스타|떡볶이|햄버거|샐러드|빵|케이크|음료|과자|간식|식사|점심|저녁|아침|맛있|배고|디저트/.test(text)) tags.push('food');
    if (/카페|커피숍|아메리카노|라떼|에스프레소/.test(text)) tags.push('cafe');
    if (/공부|시험|과제|도서관|학원|복습|예습/.test(text)) tags.push('study');
    if (/운동|헬스|달리기|요가|수영|조깅|러닝/.test(text)) tags.push('exercise');
    if (/쇼핑|옷|신발|가방|백화점|쇼핑몰|구매|샀어|샀다/.test(text)) tags.push('shopping');
    if (/친구|애들|같이|만났|어울|모임|약속|파티/.test(text)) tags.push('friend');
    if (/혼자|솔로|외로|나 혼자/.test(text)) tags.push('alone');
    if (/눈치|싸웠|화나|짜증|스트레스|미웠|싫었|갈등|불편|상처|기분 나쁜|기분나쁜|괴롭/.test(text)) tags.push('conflict');
    if (/피곤|힘들|지쳤|녹초|빡세|기력|무기력/.test(text)) tags.push('tired');
    if (/슬프|울었|눈물|속상|우울|마음이 아/.test(text)) tags.push('sad');
    if (/좋았|행복|기뻐|설레|신났|뿌듯|즐거|만족|감사|기분 좋|기분좋/.test(text)) tags.push('happy');
    if (/학교|수업|강의|선생님|교수|교실|캠퍼스/.test(text)) tags.push('school');
    if (/회사|알바|업무|야근|직장|출근|퇴근|일했|일이 많|업무가/.test(text)) tags.push('work');
    if (/좋아하|남자친구|여자친구|썸|데이트|연애|고백/.test(text)) tags.push('love');
    if (/해냈|잘됐|성공|합격|완성|이뤄|달성|끝냈/.test(text)) tags.push('achievement');
    return tags.length ? tags : ['default'];
  }

  function pickComment(char, tags, isNegative) {
    if (isNegative && char.negativePool && char.negativePool.length > 0) {
      return char.negativePool[rand(0, char.negativePool.length - 1)];
    }
    for (const tag of tags) {
      if (char.reactions[tag] && char.reactions[tag].length > 0) {
        const pool = char.reactions[tag];
        return pool[rand(0, pool.length - 1)];
      }
    }
    const pool = char.reactions.default;
    return pool[rand(0, pool.length - 1)];
  }

  // 좋아요: 0에서 시작 → 10초 동안 랜덤 증가
  function startLikeCounter(postId) {
    const owned = (typeof window.MY_CHARACTERS_COUNT === 'number') ? window.MY_CHARACTERS_COUNT : 5;
    const extra = Math.max(0, owned - 5) * 10;
    const base = 10 + extra;
    const top = Math.min(30 + extra, 150);
    const target = rand(base, top);
    let count = 0;
    const end = Date.now() + 10000;
    function tick() {
      const el = document.getElementById(`${postId}_likes`);
      if (!el || count >= target || Date.now() >= end) return;
      count++;
      el.textContent = `좋아요 ${count}개`;
      setTimeout(tick, rand(300, 1200));
    }
    setTimeout(tick, rand(200, 700));
  }

  function timeAgo(seconds) {
    if (seconds < 60) return `방금 전`;
    const m = Math.floor(seconds / 60);
    if (m < 60) return `${m}분 전`;
    return `${Math.floor(m / 60)}시간 전`;
  }

  let postCount = 0;
  let pendingPostImage = null;

  function onPostImagePicked(e) {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = () => {
      pendingPostImage = reader.result;
      const wrap = document.getElementById('postImagePreview');
      document.getElementById('postImagePreviewImg').src = pendingPostImage;
      wrap.classList.add('show');
    };
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
  }
  function clearPendingPostImage() {
    pendingPostImage = null;
    document.getElementById('postImagePreview').classList.remove('show');
    document.getElementById('postImageInput').value = '';
  }

  async function generatePostImage(text) {
    const apiKey = getApiKey();
    if (!apiKey) return null;
    try {
      const res = await fetch('https://api.openai.com/v1/images/generations', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: {
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
          'Authorization': `Bearer ${apiKey}`
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          model: 'gpt-image-1',
          prompt: `이 일기 내용을 부드럽고 파스텔 톤의 동화 같은 일러스트로 요약해줘. 사람의 얼굴이나 텍스트는 그리지 마. 일기: "${text}"`,
          size: '1024x1024',
          n: 1
        })
      });
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error('image API ' + res.status);
      const data = await res.json();
      const b64 = data.data?.[0]?.b64_json;
      if (b64) return `data:image/png;base64,${b64}`;
      const url = data.data?.[0]?.url;
      return url || null;
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn('AI 이미지 생성 실패:', e);
      return null;
    }
  }

  if (typeof window.insertSortedDesc !== 'function') {
    window.insertSortedDesc = function(feed, card) {
      const cms = Number(card.dataset.createdMs) || 0;
      const existing = feed.querySelectorAll('.post-card');
      for (const ex of existing) {
        let em = Number(ex.dataset.createdMs) || 0;
        if (!em) {
          const t = ex.querySelector('.post-time')?.textContent || '';
          const d = new Date(t).getTime();
          em = isNaN(d) ? 0 : d;
          if (em) ex.dataset.createdMs = String(em);
        }
        if (cms > em) { feed.insertBefore(card, ex); return; }
      }
      feed.appendChild(card);
    };
  }
  const insertSortedDesc = window.insertSortedDesc;

  function publishPost() {
    const input = document.getElementById('diaryInput');
    const text = input.value.trim();
    if (!text) return;

    const btn = document.getElementById('postBtn');
    btn.disabled = true;
    btn.textContent = '게시 중...';

    const tags = analyzePost(text);
    const postId = 'post_' + (++postCount);
    POST_TEXTS[postId] = text;
    const feed = document.getElementById('dynamicFeed');
    const hasApiKey = !!getApiKey();
    const attachedImage = pendingPostImage;
    const willGenerateAI = !attachedImage && hasApiKey;

    document.getElementById('emptyHint').style.display = 'none';

    let mediaHtml = '';
    if (attachedImage) {
      mediaHtml = `<img class="post-image" src="${attachedImage}" alt="">`;
    } else if (willGenerateAI) {
      mediaHtml = `<div class="post-image-placeholder-ai" id="${postId}_aimedia">이미지 생성 중… ✨</div>`;
    }

    const card = document.createElement('div');
    card.className = 'post-card post-border';
    card.id = postId;
    let maxMs = Date.now() + 3600000;
    document.querySelectorAll('#dynamicFeed .post-card').forEach(c => {
      const m = Number(c.dataset.createdMs) || 0;
      if (m > maxMs) maxMs = m;
    });
    card.dataset.createdMs = String(maxMs + 1);
    card.innerHTML = `
      <div class="post-top">
        <div class="post-avatar" data-me="1">${avatarHtml(myProfile)}</div>
        <div class="post-info">
          <div class="post-username" data-me="1">${escHtml(myProfile.username)}</div>
          <div class="post-time">방금 전</div>
        </div>
        <button class="post-menu-btn" onclick="togglePostMenu(event, '${postId}')">···</button>
        <div class="post-menu" id="${postId}_menu">
          <button onclick="pinPost('${postId}')">📌 고정</button>
          <button class="danger" onclick="deletePost('${postId}')">🗑 삭제</button>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="post-content">${escHtml(text)}</div>
      ${mediaHtml}
      <div class="post-actions">
        <button class="action-btn" onclick="toggleLike(this, '${postId}')">🤍</button>
        <button class="action-btn" onclick="focusMyPostComment('${postId}')">💬</button>
      </div>
      <div class="likes-count" id="${postId}_likes">좋아요 0개</div>
      <div class="comments" id="${postId}_comments"></div>
      <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;padding:10px 16px;border-top:1px solid #f3e3ec;align-items:center;">
        <div class="comment-avatar" style="width:28px;height:28px;">${avatarHtml(myProfile)}</div>
        <input type="text" id="${postId}_userCommentInput"
          placeholder="댓글 달기..."
          onkeydown="if(event.key==='Enter'&&!event.isComposing&&event.keyCode!==229)submitMyPostComment('${postId}')"
          style="flex:1;border:none;outline:none;background:transparent;font-size:13px;color:#333;">
        <button onclick="submitMyPostComment('${postId}')"
          class="comment-send-btn" aria-label="게시"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M12 19V5M5 12l7-7 7 7"/></svg></button>
      </div>
    `;

    insertSortedDesc(feed, card);
    input.value = '';
    btn.textContent = '게시하기';
    btn.disabled = false;
    clearPendingPostImage();

    // AI 이미지 생성 (비동기 — 댓글 흐름은 즉시 시작)
    if (willGenerateAI) {
      generatePostImage(text).then(url => {
        const placeholder = document.getElementById(`${postId}_aimedia`);
        if (!placeholder) return;
        if (url) {
          const img = document.createElement('img');
          img.className = 'post-image';
          img.src = url;
          placeholder.replaceWith(img);
        } else {
          placeholder.remove();
          showToast('AI 이미지 생성 실패 🥲');
        }
        persistState();
      });
    }

    // 좋아요 카운터 시작 (0 → 10초간 점진 증가)
    startLikeCounter(postId);
    persistState();

    // 부정 댓글 0~2개 랜덤 (0이 더 자주 나오도록 가중)
    const negCount = [0, 0, 0, 1, 1, 2][rand(0, 5)];
    const shuffled = [...CHARACTERS].sort(() => Math.random() - 0.5);
    const negCandidates = shuffled.filter(c => c.negativePool && c.negativePool.length > 0);
    const negIds = new Set(negCandidates.slice(0, negCount).map(c => c.id));

    let accDelay = 0;
    shuffled.forEach(char => {
      const isNeg = negIds.has(char.id);
      accDelay += char.delay() * 350;
      const myDelay = accDelay;

      setTimeout(() => showTyping(postId, char), Math.max(0, myDelay - 400));
      setTimeout(async () => {
        let body = await generateComment(char, text, isNeg);
        if (!body) body = pickComment(char, tags, isNeg);
        removeTyping(postId);
        addComment(postId, char, body, myDelay / 1000);
      }, myDelay);
    });
  }

  function showTyping(postId, char) {
    removeTyping(postId);
    const container = document.getElementById(`${postId}_comments`);
    if (!container) return;
    const el = document.createElement('div');
    el.className = 'typing-indicator';
    el.id = `${postId}_typing`;
    el.innerHTML = `
      <div class="comment-avatar" style="width:28px;height:28px;"><img src="${char.img}" alt="${char.id}" onerror="this.replaceWith(Object.assign(document.createElement('span'),{textContent:'${char.emoji}'}))"></div>
      <span style="font-size:12px;color:#bbb;">${char.id}</span>
      <div class="typing-dots"><span></span><span></span><span></span></div>
    `;
    container.appendChild(el);
  }

  function removeTyping(postId) {
    const el = document.getElementById(`${postId}_typing`);
    if (el) el.remove();
  }

  // ── 댓글 삭제 (DOM 기반) ──
  function removeClosestComment(el) {
    const node = el.closest('.comment, .reply');
    if (node) node.remove();
    persistState();
  }
  function deleteCharPostThreadComment(charId, idx, threadIdx) {
    const key = `${charId}-${idx}`;
    const thread = getCharPostThread(key);
    thread.splice(threadIdx, 1);
    renderCharPostThread(charId, idx);
    persistState();
  }

  // ── 기존(저장돼 있던) 내 게시글 마이그레이션: 📤 제거 + 💬 기능 + 입력바 ──
  function migrateExistingMyPosts() {
    const feed = document.getElementById('dynamicFeed');
    if (!feed) return;
    let changed = false;
    feed.querySelectorAll('.post-card').forEach(card => {
      const postId = card.id;
      if (!postId) return;
      card.querySelectorAll('.post-actions .action-btn').forEach(btn => {
        const t = btn.textContent.trim();
        if (t === '📤') { btn.remove(); changed = true; }
        else if (t === '💬' && !btn.getAttribute('onclick')) {
          btn.setAttribute('onclick', `focusMyPostComment('${postId}')`);
          changed = true;
        }
      });
      if (!card.querySelector(`#${postId}_userCommentInput`)) {
        const bar = document.createElement('div');
        bar.style.cssText = 'display:flex;gap:8px;padding:10px 16px;border-top:1px solid #f3e3ec;align-items:center;';
        bar.innerHTML = `
          <div class="comment-avatar" style="width:28px;height:28px;">${avatarHtml(myProfile)}</div>
          <input type="text" id="${postId}_userCommentInput" placeholder="댓글 달기..." onkeydown="if(event.key==='Enter'&&!event.isComposing&&event.keyCode!==229)submitMyPostComment('${postId}')" style="flex:1;border:none;outline:none;background:transparent;font-size:13px;color:#333;">
          <button onclick="submitMyPostComment('${postId}')" class="comment-send-btn" aria-label="게시"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M12 19V5M5 12l7-7 7 7"/></svg></button>
        `;
        card.appendChild(bar);
        changed = true;
      }
    });
    // 기존 댓글들에 삭제 버튼 추가
    feed.querySelectorAll('.comment .comment-time, .reply .comment-time').forEach(timeEl => {
      if (!timeEl.querySelector('.comment-delete')) {
        const del = document.createElement('span');
        del.className = 'comment-delete';
        del.setAttribute('onclick', 'removeClosestComment(this)');
        del.textContent = '삭제';
        timeEl.appendChild(del);
        changed = true;
      }
    });
    if (changed) persistState();
  }

  // ── 댓글 입력 포커스 / 내 게시글 댓글 ──
  function focusMyPostComment(postId) {
    const inp = document.getElementById(`${postId}_userCommentInput`);
    if (!inp) return;
    inp.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' });
    setTimeout(() => inp.focus({ preventScroll: true }), 300);
  }
  function focusCharPostComment(charId, idx) {
    const safeKey = (charId+'-'+idx).replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g,'_');
    const inp = document.getElementById(`cpc_input_${safeKey}`);
    if (!inp) return;
    inp.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' });
    setTimeout(() => inp.focus({ preventScroll: true }), 300);
  }
  function submitMyPostComment(postId) {
    const input = document.getElementById(`${postId}_userCommentInput`);
    if (!input) return;
    const text = input.value.trim();
    if (!text) return;
    const container = document.getElementById(`${postId}_comments`);
    if (!container) return;
    const cid = `cmt_${++_commentSeq}`;
    const el = document.createElement('div');
    el.className = 'comment';
    el.id = cid;
    el.innerHTML = `
      <div class="comment-avatar">${avatarHtml(myProfile)}</div>
      <div class="comment-body">
        <span class="comment-name">${escHtml(myProfile.username)} </span>
        <span class="comment-text">${escHtml(text)}</span>
        <div class="comment-time">방금 전<span class="comment-delete" onclick="removeClosestComment(this)">삭제</span></div>
      </div>
    `;
    container.appendChild(el);
    input.value = '';
    persistState();

    // 캐릭터 중 한 명이 답글 (60% 확률, 랜덤 캐릭터)
    if (Math.random() < 0.6) {
      const char = CHARACTERS[Math.floor(Math.random() * CHARACTERS.length)];
      const postText = POST_TEXTS[postId] || '';
      setTimeout(async () => {
        let reply = null;
        try { reply = await generateCommentReply(char, postText, text); } catch(e) {}
        if (!reply) reply = pickReplyTemplate(char, text);
        // 내 댓글에 대한 답글 형태로 추가
        const rid = `cmt_${++_commentSeq}`;
        const rEl = document.createElement('div');
        rEl.className = 'comment';
        rEl.id = rid;
        rEl.innerHTML = `
          <div class="comment-avatar"><img src="${char.img}" alt="${char.id}" onerror="this.replaceWith(Object.assign(document.createElement('span'),{textContent:'${char.emoji}'}))"></div>
          <div class="comment-body">
            <span class="comment-name">${char.id} </span>
            <span class="comment-text">${escHtml(reply)}</span>
            <div class="comment-time">방금 전<span class="comment-delete" onclick="removeClosestComment(this)">삭제</span></div>
          </div>
        `;
        container.appendChild(rEl);
        persistState();
      }, 1500 + Math.random() * 2000);
    }
  }

  // ── Post text store (for reply context) ──
  const POST_TEXTS = {};
  // 캐릭터 게시글에 내가 단 댓글 + 캐릭터의 답글 저장
  const CHAR_POST_THREADS = {}; // key "charId-idx" → [{from:'me'|'char', text, ts}]
  function getCharPostThread(key) {
    if (!CHAR_POST_THREADS[key]) CHAR_POST_THREADS[key] = [];
    return CHAR_POST_THREADS[key];
  }
  function renderCharPostThread(charId, idx) {
    const key = `${charId}-${idx}`;
    const safeKey = key.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g,'_');
    const list = document.getElementById(`cpc_list_${safeKey}`);
    if (!list) return;
    const thread = getCharPostThread(key);
    const char = findChar(charId);
    list.innerHTML = thread.map((c, tIdx) => {
      const isMe = c.from === 'me';
      const avatar = isMe
        ? `<div class="comment-avatar">${avatarHtml(myProfile)}</div>`
        : `<div class="comment-avatar"><img src="${char?.img}" alt="${charId}" onerror="this.replaceWith(Object.assign(document.createElement('span'),{textContent:'${char?.emoji||'💗'}'}))"></div>`;
      const name = isMe ? escHtml(myProfile.username) : charId;
      const replies = c.replies || [];
      const repliesHtml = replies.length ? `
        <div class="replies">
          ${replies.map((r, rIdx) => {
            const rIsMe = r.from === 'me';
            const rAvatar = rIsMe
              ? `<div class="comment-avatar">${avatarHtml(myProfile)}</div>`
              : `<div class="comment-avatar"><img src="${char?.img}" alt="${charId}" onerror="this.replaceWith(Object.assign(document.createElement('span'),{textContent:'${char?.emoji||'💗'}'}))"></div>`;
            const rName = rIsMe ? escHtml(myProfile.username) : charId;
            return `
              <div class="reply${rIsMe ? ' me' : ''}">
                ${rAvatar}
                <div class="comment-body">
                  <span class="comment-name">${rName} </span>
                  <span class="comment-text">${escHtml(r.text)}</span>
                  <div class="comment-time">방금 전<span class="comment-delete" onclick="deleteCharPostThreadReply('${charId}',${idx},${tIdx},${rIdx})">삭제</span></div>
                </div>
              </div>
            `;
          }).join('')}
        </div>
      ` : '';
      return `
        <div class="comment">
          ${avatar}
          <div class="comment-body">
            <span class="comment-name">${name} </span>
            <span class="comment-text">${escHtml(c.text)}</span>
            <div class="comment-time">방금 전<span class="comment-delete" onclick="deleteCharPostThreadComment('${charId}',${idx},${tIdx})">삭제</span></div>
            ${repliesHtml}
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
    }).join('');
  }

  function deleteCharPostThreadReply(charId, idx, tIdx, rIdx) {
    const thread = getCharPostThread(`${charId}-${idx}`);
    if (!thread[tIdx] || !thread[tIdx].replies) return;
    thread[tIdx].replies.splice(rIdx, 1);
    renderCharPostThread(charId, idx);
    persistState();
  }
  async function submitCharPostComment(charId, idx) {
    const key = `${charId}-${idx}`;
    const safeKey = key.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g,'_');
    const input = document.getElementById(`cpc_input_${safeKey}`);
    if (!input) return;
    const text = input.value.trim();
    if (!text) return;
    const thread = getCharPostThread(key);
    thread.push({ from: 'me', text, ts: Date.now() });
    const tIdx = thread.length - 1;
    input.value = '';
    renderCharPostThread(charId, idx);
    persistState();

    const ctx = { postKey: key, tIdx, postIdx: idx };
    gateUserInput(charId, text, () => runCharPostReply(charId, idx, key, tIdx), 'charPost', ctx);
  }
  async function runCharPostReply(charId, idx, key, tIdx) {
    // 60% 확률로 공주가 답글 — 메인 화면의 .reply 들여쓰기 스타일로 내 댓글 아래에 추가
    if (Math.random() < 0.6) {
      const char = findChar(charId);
      const post = CHARACTER_PROFILES[charId]?.posts[idx];
      if (!char) return;
      const thread = getCharPostThread(key);
      const text = thread[tIdx]?.text || '';
      setTimeout(async () => {
        let reply = null;
        if (getApiKey() && char.systemPrompt) {
          const tone = char.replyProfile?.tone || '';
          const sys = char.systemPrompt + `\n[추가 지시 — 자기 게시글 댓글 답글 모드]\n친구 princess_chae가 너의 게시글에 댓글을 달았어. 너는 게시글 작성자로서 짧게 반응해. 톤: ${tone}\n- 한 줄, 최대 50자\n- 본문만, 따옴표 금지`;
          const out = await aiChat({
            system: sys,
            user: `너의 게시글: "${post?.caption || ''}"\nprincess_chae 댓글: "${text}"\n위 댓글에 답글을 써. 본문만.`,
            temperature: 0.95, maxTokens: 60
          });
          if (out) {
            reply = out.replace(/^["'`「」]+|["'`「」]+$/g,'').trim();
            if (reply.length > 60) reply = reply.slice(0,60).replace(/\s+\S*$/,'') + '…';
          }
        }
        if (!reply) reply = pickReplyTemplate(char);
        if (!thread[tIdx]) return;
        if (!thread[tIdx].replies) thread[tIdx].replies = [];
        thread[tIdx].replies.push({ from: 'char', text: reply, ts: Date.now() });
        renderCharPostThread(charId, idx);
        persistState();
      }, 1500 + Math.random() * 2500);
    }
  }
  let _commentSeq = 0;

  // ── 영구 저장 (localStorage) ──
  const STORAGE_KEY = 'princessgram_state_v1';
  let _saveTimer = null;
  function _doPersist() {
    try {
      const feed = document.getElementById('dynamicFeed');
      const emptyHint = document.getElementById('emptyHint');
      const data = {
        feedHtml: feed ? feed.innerHTML : '',
        postTexts: POST_TEXTS,
        postCount: typeof postCount !== 'undefined' ? postCount : 0,
        commentSeq: _commentSeq,
        dmThreads: DM_THREADS,
        charPostThreads: CHAR_POST_THREADS,
        emptyHintHidden: emptyHint ? emptyHint.style.display === 'none' : false,
        moderationMute: _moderationMute,
        charPostLikes: CHAR_POST_LIKES
      };
      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(data));
      console.log('[persist] saved', Object.keys(data));
    } catch (e) { console.warn('[persist] failed', e); }
  }
  function persistState() {
    clearTimeout(_saveTimer);
    _saveTimer = setTimeout(_doPersist, 200);
  }
  function flushPersist() {
    if (_saveTimer) { clearTimeout(_saveTimer); _saveTimer = null; }
    _doPersist();
  }
  window.addEventListener('beforeunload', flushPersist);
  window.addEventListener('pagehide', flushPersist);
  function restoreState() {
    try {
      const raw = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
      if (!raw) return;
      const data = JSON.parse(raw);
      const feed = document.getElementById('dynamicFeed');
      if (feed && data.feedHtml) {
        feed.innerHTML = data.feedHtml;
        let migrated = false;
        feed.querySelectorAll('.post-card').forEach((card, idx) => {
          card.querySelectorAll('.post-actions span').forEach(s => {
            if ((s.textContent || '').includes('🔖')) { s.remove(); migrated = true; }
          });
          card.querySelectorAll('button').forEach(b => {
            if ((b.textContent || '').trim() === '게시') {
              b.className = 'comment-send-btn';
              b.setAttribute('aria-label', '게시');
              b.removeAttribute('style');
              b.innerHTML = '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M12 19V5M5 12l7-7 7 7"/></svg>';
              migrated = true;
            }
          });
          if (!card.dataset.createdMs) {
            const t = card.querySelector('.post-time')?.textContent || '';
            const parsed = new Date(t).getTime();
            card.dataset.createdMs = String(
              !isNaN(parsed) && parsed > 0 ? parsed : (Date.now() - idx * 60000)
            );
            migrated = true;
          }
        });
        if (migrated) setTimeout(persistState, 0);
      }
      if (data.postTexts) Object.assign(POST_TEXTS, data.postTexts);
      if (typeof data.postCount === 'number') postCount = data.postCount;
      if (typeof data.commentSeq === 'number') _commentSeq = data.commentSeq;
      if (data.dmThreads) Object.assign(DM_THREADS, data.dmThreads);
      if (data.charPostThreads) Object.assign(CHAR_POST_THREADS, data.charPostThreads);
      if (data.moderationMute && typeof data.moderationMute === 'object') _moderationMute = data.moderationMute;
      if (data.charPostLikes && typeof data.charPostLikes === 'object') Object.assign(CHAR_POST_LIKES, data.charPostLikes);
      const emptyHint = document.getElementById('emptyHint');
      if (emptyHint && data.emptyHintHidden) emptyHint.style.display = 'none';
      updateDMBadge();
    } catch (e) { console.warn('restore failed', e); }
  }

  function addComment(postId, char, text, elapsedSec) {
    const container = document.getElementById(`${postId}_comments`);
    if (!container) return;
    const cid = `cmt_${++_commentSeq}`;
    const el = document.createElement('div');
    el.className = 'comment';
    el.id = cid;
    el.innerHTML = `
      <div class="comment-avatar"><img src="${char.img}" alt="${char.id}" onerror="this.replaceWith(Object.assign(document.createElement('span'),{textContent:'${char.emoji}'}))"></div>
      <div class="comment-body">
        <span class="comment-name">${char.id} </span>
        <span class="comment-text">${escHtml(text)}</span>
        <div class="comment-time">${timeAgo(Math.round(elapsedSec))}<span class="comment-delete" onclick="removeClosestComment(this)">삭제</span></div>
        <button class="reply-btn" onclick="openReplyInput('${postId}','${cid}','${char.id}')">답글 달기</button>
        <div class="reply-input-wrap" id="${cid}_replyWrap">
          <input type="text" id="${cid}_replyInput" placeholder="@${char.id} 에게 답글..." onkeydown="if(event.key==='Enter'&&!event.isComposing&&event.keyCode!==229)submitReply('${postId}','${cid}','${char.id}')">
          <button onclick="submitReply('${postId}','${cid}','${char.id}')">등록</button>
        </div>
        <div class="replies" id="${cid}_replies"></div>
      </div>
    `;
    container.appendChild(el);
    persistState();
  }

  function openReplyInput(postId, cid, charId) {
    const wrap = document.getElementById(`${cid}_replyWrap`);
    if (!wrap) return;
    wrap.classList.toggle('open');
    if (wrap.classList.contains('open')) {
      document.getElementById(`${cid}_replyInput`)?.focus();
    }
  }

  function findChar(charId) {
    return CHARACTERS.find(c => c.id === charId);
  }

  function appendReplyToThread(cid, who, text, charObj) {
    const wrap = document.getElementById(`${cid}_replies`);
    if (!wrap) return;
    const el = document.createElement('div');
    el.className = 'reply' + (who === 'me' ? ' me' : '');
    const avatar = who === 'me'
      ? `<div class="comment-avatar">${avatarHtml(myProfile)}</div>`
      : `<div class="comment-avatar"><img src="${charObj.img}" alt="${charObj.id}" onerror="this.replaceWith(Object.assign(document.createElement('span'),{textContent:'${charObj.emoji}'}))"></div>`;
    const name = who === 'me' ? escHtml(myProfile.username) : charObj.id;
    el.innerHTML = `
      ${avatar}
      <div class="comment-body">
        <span class="comment-name">${name} </span>
        <span class="comment-text">${escHtml(text)}</span>
        <div class="comment-time">방금 전<span class="comment-delete" onclick="removeClosestComment(this)">삭제</span></div>
      </div>
    `;
    wrap.appendChild(el);
    persistState();
  }

  function submitReply(postId, cid, charId) {
    console.log('[reply] submitReply', { postId, cid, charId });
    const input = document.getElementById(`${cid}_replyInput`);
    if (!input) { console.warn('[reply] no input'); return; }
    const text = input.value.trim();
    if (!text) { console.warn('[reply] empty'); return; }
    const char = findChar(charId);
    if (!char) { console.warn('[reply] no char', charId); return; }
    appendReplyToThread(cid, 'me', text, null);
    input.value = '';
    document.getElementById(`${cid}_replyWrap`).classList.remove('open');

    gateUserInput(charId, text, () => runUserReplyResponse(char, charId, postId, cid, text), 'reply', { cid, char });
  }
  function runUserReplyResponse(char, charId, postId, cid, text) {
    const postText = POST_TEXTS[postId] || '';
    const inlineProb = (char.replyProfile && char.replyProfile.inlineReplyChance) ?? 0.45;
    const rollInline = Math.random() < inlineProb;
    console.log('[reply] decision', char.id, 'inlineProb=', inlineProb, 'rollInline=', rollInline);

    if (rollInline) {
      const delay = 1500 + Math.random() * 1500;
      console.log('[reply] inline reply in', delay, 'ms');
      setTimeout(async () => {
        let reply;
        try { reply = await generateCommentReply(char, postText, text); }
        catch (e) { console.warn('[reply] gen err', e); }
        if (!reply) reply = pickReplyTemplate(char, text);
        console.log('[reply] inline appending:', reply);
        appendReplyToThread(cid, 'them', reply, char);
      }, delay);
    } else {
      const delay = 2500 + Math.random() * 2500;
      console.log('[reply] DM in', delay, 'ms');
      setTimeout(() => {
        sendDMFromChar(char, { postText, userReply: text, kind: 'after_reply' });
      }, delay);
    }
  }

  function toggleLike(btn, postId) {
    const likesEl = document.getElementById(`${postId}_likes`)
      || btn.closest('.post-card').querySelector('.likes-count');
    const n = parseInt(likesEl.textContent.replace(/[^0-9]/g, '')) || 0;
    if (btn.textContent === '🤍') {
      btn.textContent = '❤️';
      likesEl.textContent = `좋아요 ${n + 1}개`;
    } else {
      btn.textContent = '🤍';
      likesEl.textContent = `좋아요 ${Math.max(0, n - 1)}개`;
    }
    persistState();
  }

  // 캐릭터 게시글 좋아요 상태 (charId:idx → {liked, count})
  const CHAR_POST_LIKES = {};
  // DB post id 기반 좋아요 — 메인피드와 프로필에서 공유
  function _hashStr(s) { let h = 0; for (let i = 0; i < s.length; i++) { h = ((h<<5)-h) + s.charCodeAt(i); h |= 0; } return Math.abs(h); }
  function getLikeCountByPostId(postId) {
    if (!postId) return 0;
    if (!CHAR_POST_LIKES[postId]) {
      // 최초 진입 시 안정적인 기본 카운트(post id 해시 기반 30~330)
      CHAR_POST_LIKES[postId] = { liked: false, count: 30 + (_hashStr(postId) % 300) };
    }
    return CHAR_POST_LIKES[postId].count;
  }
  function isLikedByPostId(postId) {
    return !!(postId && CHAR_POST_LIKES[postId]?.liked);
  }
  function toggleLikeByPostId(btn, postId) {
    if (!postId) return;
    getLikeCountByPostId(postId);
    const rec = CHAR_POST_LIKES[postId];
    rec.liked = !rec.liked;
    rec.count += rec.liked ? 1 : -1;
    if (rec.count < 0) rec.count = 0;
    // 같은 postId를 가진 모든 카드(메인피드/프로필) 동기화
    document.querySelectorAll(`[data-post-id="${postId}"]`).forEach(card => {
      const btnEl = card.querySelector('.post-actions .action-btn');
      const cntEl = card.querySelector('.likes-count');
      if (btnEl) btnEl.textContent = rec.liked ? '❤️' : '🤍';
      if (cntEl) cntEl.textContent = `좋아요 ${rec.count}개`;
    });
    if (btn) btn.textContent = rec.liked ? '❤️' : '🤍';
    try { persistState(); } catch {}
  }

  function toggleCharPostLike(btn, charId, idx) {
    const key = `${charId}:${idx}`;
    const likesEl = btn.closest('.post-card').querySelector('.likes-count');
    const n = parseInt(likesEl.textContent.replace(/[^0-9]/g, '')) || 0;
    const liked = btn.textContent === '❤️';
    if (liked) {
      btn.textContent = '🤍';
      likesEl.textContent = `좋아요 ${Math.max(0, n - 1)}개`;
      CHAR_POST_LIKES[key] = { liked: false, count: Math.max(0, n - 1) };
    } else {
      btn.textContent = '❤️';
      likesEl.textContent = `좋아요 ${n + 1}개`;
      CHAR_POST_LIKES[key] = { liked: true, count: n + 1 };
    }
    persistState();
  }

  function escHtml(str) {
    return str.replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;').replace(/"/g,'&quot;');
  }

  // ── Navigation ──
  function hideAllPages() {
    document.getElementById('homePage').style.display = 'none';
    document.getElementById('messengerPage').classList.remove('open');
    document.getElementById('dmChatView')?.classList.remove('open');
    document.body.classList.remove('dm-chat-open');
    _openDMCharId = null;
    document.getElementById('profilePage').classList.remove('open');
    document.getElementById('profileEditPage').classList.remove('open');
    document.getElementById('characterProfilePage').classList.remove('open');
    document.getElementById('characterPostPage').classList.remove('open');
  }
  function goMessenger() {
    hideAllPages();
    document.getElementById('messengerPage').classList.add('open');
    document.getElementById('dmChatView').classList.remove('open');
    document.getElementById('dmListView').style.display = '';
    _openDMCharId = null;
    const headerName = document.getElementById('dmHeaderUsername');
    if (headerName) headerName.textContent = myProfile.username || 'princess_chae';
    renderDMList();
  }
  function goHome() {
    hideAllPages();
    document.getElementById('homePage').style.display = 'flex';
  }
  function goProfile() {
    hideAllPages();
    document.getElementById('profilePage').classList.add('open');
    renderProfileView();
  }

  // 프로필 ⋯ 메뉴 + 로그아웃
  function toggleProfileMenu(e) {
    if (e) e.stopPropagation();
    const menu = document.getElementById('profileMenu');
    if (!menu) return;
    const open = menu.style.display === 'block';
    menu.style.display = open ? 'none' : 'block';
    if (!open) {
      setTimeout(() => {
        document.addEventListener('click', _closeProfileMenuOnce, { once: true });
      }, 0);
    }
  }
  function _closeProfileMenuOnce() {
    const menu = document.getElementById('profileMenu');
    if (menu) menu.style.display = 'none';
  }
  async function logoutUser() {
    const menu = document.getElementById('profileMenu');
    if (menu) menu.style.display = 'none';
    if (!confirm('로그아웃 할까요? 게시물·DM 등 모든 기록은 그대로 보관돼요.')) return;
    try {
      const csrfRes = await fetch('/api/auth/csrf', { credentials: 'same-origin' });
      const { csrfToken } = await csrfRes.json();
      await fetch('/api/auth/signout', {
        method: 'POST',
        credentials: 'same-origin',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded' },
        body: new URLSearchParams({ csrfToken, callbackUrl: '/login' })
      });
    } catch (e) { console.warn('[auth] signout request failed', e); }
    // 다른 사용자가 같은 브라우저로 로그인할 때 옛 캐시가 보이지 않도록 클리어
    try {
      localStorage.removeItem('princess_profile');
      localStorage.removeItem('princessgram_state_v1');
      localStorage.removeItem('persona_summary_last');
    } catch (e) {}
    window.location.href = '/login';
  }
  function goProfileEdit() {
    hideAllPages();
    document.getElementById('profileEditPage').classList.add('open');
    fillProfileForm();
  }
