```html
  function shuffle(arr) {
    const a = arr.slice();
    for (let i = a.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
      const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));
      [a[i], a[j]] = [a[j], a[i]];
    }
    return a;
  }

  function goCharacterProfile(charId) {
    const p = CHARACTER_PROFILES[charId];
    if (!p) return;
    hideAllPages();
    const page = document.getElementById('characterProfilePage');
    page.classList.add('open');
    page.innerHTML = `
      <div class="ig-profile-topbar">
        <span class="ig-profile-username">${charId}</span>
        <span class="ig-profile-settings" onclick="goHome()" title="홈으로">✕</span>
      </div>
      <div class="ig-profile-head">
        <div class="ig-profile-avatar"><img src="${p.img}" alt="${charId}"></div>
        <div class="ig-profile-stats">
          <div class="ig-stat"><div class="ig-stat-num">${p.posts.length}</div><div class="ig-stat-label">게시물</div></div>
          <div class="ig-stat"><div class="ig-stat-num">${p.grade}</div><div class="ig-stat-label">공주 등급</div></div>
          <div class="ig-stat"><div class="ig-stat-num">${p.friends}</div><div class="ig-stat-label">친구들</div></div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="ig-profile-bio">
        <div class="ig-profile-name">${p.name}</div>
        <div class="ig-profile-desc" style="white-space:pre-line;">${escHtml(p.bio)}</div>
      </div>
      <div class="ig-profile-actions">
        <button class="ig-btn" onclick="showToast('팔로우했어요 💕')">팔로우</button>
        <button class="ig-btn" onclick="startDMWith('${charId}')">메시지</button>
      </div>
      <div class="ig-profile-tabs"><div class="ig-tab active">▦ 게시물</div></div>
      <div class="ig-profile-grid" id="charProfileGrid_${charId}"></div>
    `;
    const grid = document.getElementById(`charProfileGrid_${charId}`);
    const [c1, c2] = p.theme;
    p.posts.forEach((post, idx) => {
      const item = document.createElement('div');
      item.className = 'ig-grid-item';
      if (post.image) {
        item.style.cssText = 'cursor:pointer;overflow:hidden;position:relative;';
        const isVid = /^data:video\//.test(post.image) || /\.(mp4|webm|mov)(\?|$)/i.test(post.image);
        item.innerHTML = isVid
          ? `<video src="${post.image}" muted playsinline style="width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:cover;display:block;"></video><span style="position:absolute;inset:0;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;color:#fff;font-size:28px;text-shadow:0 2px 6px rgba(0,0,0,0.5);pointer-events:none;">▶</span>`
          : `<img src="${post.image}" alt="" style="width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:cover;display:block;">`;
      } else {
        item.style.cssText = `background:linear-gradient(135deg,${c1},${c2});display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-size:42px;cursor:pointer;`;
        item.textContent = post.emoji;
      }
      item.onclick = () => goCharacterPost(charId, idx);
      grid.appendChild(item);
    });
  }

  function renderCharPostCard(charId, post, i, p) {
    const [c1, c2] = p.theme;
    const comments = randomCommentsFor(charId, post);
    const safeId = (charId+'-'+i).replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g,'_');
    const dbId = post.__db && post.id ? post.id : null;
    const dbAttr = dbId ? ` data-post-id="${dbId}"` : '';
    return `
      <div class="post-card post-border" id="charPost_${charId}_${i}"${dbAttr} style="margin-bottom:16px;">
        <div class="post-top">
          <div class="post-avatar"><img src="${p.img}" alt="${charId}" style="width:100%;height:100%;border-radius:50%;object-fit:cover;"></div>
          <div class="post-info">
            <div class="post-username">${charId}</div>
            <div class="post-time">${Math.floor(Math.random()*23)+1}시간 전</div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="post-content">${escHtml(post.caption)}</div>
        ${(post.images && post.images.length > 1)
          ? `<div class="post-carousel" style="position:relative;width:100%;aspect-ratio:1/1;overflow:hidden;">
              <div class="carousel-track" style="display:flex;width:100%;height:100%;transition:transform 0.3s ease;transform:translateX(0%);">
                ${post.images.map(src => {
                  const v = /^data:video\//.test(src) || /\.(mp4|webm|mov)(\?|$)/i.test(src);
                  return v
                    ? `<video src="${src}" controls playsinline style="width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:cover;flex-shrink:0;display:block;"></video>`
                    : `<img src="${src}" alt="" style="width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:cover;flex-shrink:0;display:block;">`;
                }).join('')}
              </div>
              <button onclick="carouselMove(this,-1,${post.images.length})" style="position:absolute;left:8px;top:50%;transform:translateY(-50%);background:rgba(255,255,255,0.85);border:none;width:32px;height:32px;border-radius:50%;font-size:18px;cursor:pointer;box-shadow:0 2px 6px rgba(0,0,0,0.2);">‹</button>
              <button onclick="carouselMove(this,1,${post.images.length})" style="position:absolute;right:8px;top:50%;transform:translateY(-50%);background:rgba(255,255,255,0.85);border:none;width:32px;height:32px;border-radius:50%;font-size:18px;cursor:pointer;box-shadow:0 2px 6px rgba(0,0,0,0.2);">›</button>
              <div class="carousel-dots" data-idx="0" style="position:absolute;bottom:10px;left:50%;transform:translateX(-50%);display:flex;gap:6px;">
                ${post.images.map((_,k) => `<span style="width:6px;height:6px;border-radius:50%;background:${k===0?'#fff':'rgba(255,255,255,0.5)'};box-shadow:0 1px 2px rgba(0,0,0,0.3);"></span>`).join('')}
              </div>
            </div>`
          : post.image
            ? ((/^data:video\//.test(post.image) || /\.(mp4|webm|mov)(\?|$)/i.test(post.image))
                ? `<video class="post-image" src="${post.image}" controls playsinline style="width:100%;aspect-ratio:1/1;object-fit:cover;display:block;background:#000;"></video>`
                : `<img class="post-image" src="${post.image}" alt="" style="width:100%;aspect-ratio:1/1;object-fit:cover;display:block;">`)
            : `<div class="post-image" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,${c1},${c2});display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-size:120px;aspect-ratio:1/1;">${post.emoji}</div>`}
        <div class="post-actions">
          ${dbId
            ? `<button class="action-btn" onclick="toggleLikeByPostId(this, '${dbId}')">${isLikedByPostId(dbId) ? '❤️' : '🤍'}</button>`
            : `<button class="action-btn" onclick="toggleCharPostLike(this,'${charId}',${i})">${CHAR_POST_LIKES[`${charId}:${i}`]?.liked ? '❤️' : '🤍'}</button>`}
          <button class="action-btn" onclick="focusCharPostComment('${charId}',${i})">💬</button>
        </div>
        <div class="likes-count">좋아요 ${dbId ? getLikeCountByPostId(dbId) : (() => { const k = `${charId}:${i}`; if (CHAR_POST_LIKES[k]) return CHAR_POST_LIKES[k].count; return charId === 'ruby_' ? (Math.floor(Math.random()*701)+100) : (Math.floor(Math.random()*200)+30); })()}개</div>
        <div class="comments">
          ${comments.map(c => `
            <div class="comment">
              <div class="comment-avatar"><img src="${c.img}" alt="${c.id}" style="width:100%;height:100%;border-radius:50%;object-fit:cover;"></div>
              <div class="comment-body">
                <span class="comment-name">${c.id}</span>
                <span class="comment-text">${escHtml(c.text)}</span>
                <div class="comment-time">${c.time}분 전<span class="comment-delete" onclick="removeClosestComment(this)">삭제</span></div>
              </div>
            </div>
          `).join('')}
          <div id="cpc_list_${safeId}"></div>
        </div>
        <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;padding:10px 16px;border-top:1px solid #f3e3ec;background:#fff;align-items:center;">
          <div class="comment-avatar" style="width:28px;height:28px;">${avatarHtml(myProfile)}</div>
          <input type="text" id="cpc_input_${safeId}"
            placeholder="댓글 달기..."
            onkeydown="if(event.key==='Enter'&&!event.isComposing&&event.keyCode!==229)submitCharPostComment('${charId}',${i})"
            style="flex:1;border:none;outline:none;background:transparent;font-size:13px;color:#333;">
          <button onclick="submitCharPostComment('${charId}',${i})"
            class="comment-send-btn" aria-label="게시"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M12 19V5M5 12l7-7 7 7"/></svg></button>
        </div>
      </div>
    `;
  }

  function goCharacterPost(charId, idx) {
    const p = CHARACTER_PROFILES[charId];
    if (!p || !p.posts[idx]) return;
    hideAllPages();
    const page = document.getElementById('characterPostPage');
    page.classList.add('open');
    page.innerHTML = `
      <div style="position:sticky;top:48px;z-index:50;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;background:#fff;border-bottom:1px solid #f3e3ec;padding:12px 16px;margin:-16px -16px 12px;">
        <button onclick="goCharacterProfile('${charId}')" style="background:none;border:none;font-size:22px;cursor:pointer;color:#d4548a;">←</button>
        <span style="font-weight:600;color:#333;">게시물</span>
      </div>
      ${p.posts.map((post, i) => renderCharPostCard(charId, post, i, p)).join('')}
    `;
    p.posts.forEach((_, i) => renderCharPostThread(charId, i));
    // DB 게시글: 저장된 댓글을 즉시 표시 (애니메이션 없음 — 메인피드 신규 게시글에서만 staggered)
    p.posts.forEach((post, i) => {
      if (!post.__db || !Array.isArray(post.__dbComments) || !post.__dbComments.length) return;
      const card = document.getElementById(`charPost_${charId}_${i}`);
      if (!card) return;
      const commentsBox = card.querySelector('.comments');
      const safeId = (charId+'-'+i).replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g,'_');
      const anchor = document.getElementById(`cpc_list_${safeId}`);
      post.__dbComments.forEach((c) => {
        const prof = CHARACTER_PROFILES[c.id];
        if (!prof || !commentsBox) return;
        const el = document.createElement('div');
        el.className = 'comment';
        el.innerHTML = `
          <div class="comment-avatar"><img src="${prof.img}" alt="${c.id}" style="width:100%;height:100%;border-radius:50%;object-fit:cover;"></div>
          <div class="comment-body">
            <span class="comment-name">${c.id}</span>
            <span class="comment-text">${escHtml(c.text)}</span>
            <div class="comment-time">방금<span class="comment-delete" onclick="removeClosestComment(this)">삭제</span></div>
          </div>
        `;
        if (anchor) commentsBox.insertBefore(el, anchor);
        else commentsBox.appendChild(el);
      });
    });
    const target = document.getElementById(`charPost_${charId}_${idx}`);
    if (target) target.scrollIntoView({block:'start'});
  }

  function carouselMove(btn, dir, total) {
    const carousel = btn.closest('.post-carousel');
    const track = carousel.querySelector('.carousel-track');
    const dots = carousel.querySelector('.carousel-dots');
    let idx = parseInt(dots.dataset.idx || '0', 10);
    idx = Math.max(0, Math.min(total - 1, idx + dir));
    track.style.transform = `translateX(-${idx * 100}%)`;
    dots.dataset.idx = idx;
    [...dots.children].forEach((d, i) => {
      d.style.background = i === idx ? '#fff' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.5)';
    });
  }

  // Touch swipe for carousel
  document.addEventListener('touchstart', e => {
    const c = e.target.closest('.post-carousel');
    if (c) c.dataset.touchX = e.touches[0].clientX;
  }, {passive:true});
  document.addEventListener('touchend', e => {
    const c = e.target.closest('.post-carousel');
    if (!c || !c.dataset.touchX) return;
    const dx = e.changedTouches[0].clientX - parseFloat(c.dataset.touchX);
    if (Math.abs(dx) > 40) {
      const total = c.querySelectorAll('.carousel-track img').length;
      const btn = c.querySelector('button');
      carouselMove(btn, dx < 0 ? 1 : -1, total);
    }
    delete c.dataset.touchX;
  }, {passive:true});

  function randomCommentsFor(charId, post) {
    // DB 게시글이면 초기 렌더 시 댓글을 비워두고, 나중에 staggered 애니메이션으로 주입
    if (post && post.__db) return [];
    if (post && Array.isArray(post.comments) && post.comments.length) {
      return post.comments.map(c => ({
        id: c.id,
        img: CHARACTER_PROFILES[c.id]?.img,
        text: c.text,
        time: Math.floor(Math.random() * 50) + 1
      }));
    }
    const others = Object.keys(CHARACTER_PROFILES).filter(id => id !== charId && id !== 'pearl_diary');
    const count = 3 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 3); // 3~5
    const picked = shuffle(others).slice(0, Math.min(count, others.length));
    return picked.map(id => {
      const pool = COMMENT_POOL[id] || ['좋아 💕'];
      return {
        id,
        img: CHARACTER_PROFILES[id].img,
        text: pool[Math.floor(Math.random() * pool.length)],
        time: Math.floor(Math.random() * 50) + 1
      };
    });
  }

  // ── IG profile view rendering ──
  const ZODIAC_KO = ['원숭이','닭','개','돼지','쥐','소','호랑이','토끼','용','뱀','말','양'];
  function buildBioLine(p) {
    const parts = [];
    if (p.mbti) parts.push(p.mbti);
    if (p.birthDate) {
      const [y,m,d] = p.birthDate.split('-').map(Number);
      if (y) parts.push(ZODIAC_KO[y % 12] + '띠');
      if (m && d && typeof getStarSign === 'function') {
        try { parts.push(getStarSign(m, d)[0]); } catch(e){}
      }
    }
    if (p.birthTime && typeof getSijin === 'function') {
      const s = getSijin(p.birthTime);
      if (s) parts.push(s[0]);
    }
    return parts.join(' · ');
  }

  function renderProfileView() {
    const av = document.getElementById('igProfileAvatar');
    av.innerHTML = myProfile.avatarDataUrl
      ? `<img src="${myProfile.avatarDataUrl}" alt="me">`
      : '👸';
    const _ign = document.getElementById('igProfileUsername'); if (_ign) _ign.textContent = myProfile.username;
    document.getElementById('igProfileName').textContent = myProfile.name || myProfile.username;
    document.getElementById('igProfileDesc').textContent = myProfile.bio || '';
    const cards = document.querySelectorAll('#dynamicFeed .post-card:not([id^="feedCharPost_"]):not([data-post-id])');
    document.getElementById('igStatPosts').textContent = cards.length;
    document.getElementById('igStatGrade').textContent = princessGradeEmoji(cards.length);
    const friendCount = (typeof window.MY_CHARACTERS_COUNT === 'number')
      ? window.MY_CHARACTERS_COUNT
      : (Array.isArray(window.CHARACTERS) ? window.CHARACTERS.length : 5);
    document.getElementById('igStatFriends').textContent = friendCount;
    renderProfileGrid(cards);
  }

  window.MY_CHARACTERS_COUNT = 5;
  async function refreshMyCharactersCount() {
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/my/characters', { credentials: 'same-origin' });
      if (!res.ok) return;
      const j = await res.json();
      if (j.ok && typeof j.count === 'number') {
        window.MY_CHARACTERS_COUNT = j.count;
        const el = document.getElementById('igStatFriends');
        if (el) el.textContent = j.count;
      }
    } catch {}
  }
  setTimeout(refreshMyCharactersCount, 400);

  function openInviteModal() {
    const m = document.getElementById('inviteMsg'); if (m) { m.textContent = ''; m.classList.remove('error'); }
    const i = document.getElementById('inviteCodeInput'); if (i) i.value = '';
    document.getElementById('inviteModal').classList.add('show');
    setTimeout(() => i?.focus(), 50);
  }
  function closeInviteModal() {
    document.getElementById('inviteModal').classList.remove('show');
  }
  async function submitInviteCode() {
    const input = document.getElementById('inviteCodeInput');
    const msg = document.getElementById('inviteMsg');
    const btn = document.getElementById('inviteSubmitBtn');
    const code = (input.value || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    msg.classList.remove('error'); msg.textContent = '';
    if (code.length < 4) { msg.textContent = '코드를 정확히 입력해주세요'; msg.classList.add('error'); return; }
    btn.disabled = true;
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/invite/redeem', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        credentials: 'same-origin',
        body: JSON.stringify({ code })
      });
      const j = await res.json();
      if (res.ok && j.ok) {
        closeInviteModal();
        playInviteBurst(j.princess);
        await refreshMyCharactersCount();
        try {
          if (typeof window.reloadCharacterProfiles === 'function') await window.reloadCharacterProfiles();
        } catch {}
      } else if (res.status === 409) {
        msg.textContent = '이미 함께하고 있는 공주예요 🌸';
        msg.classList.add('error');
      } else if (res.status === 404) {
        msg.textContent = '이 코드는 사용할 수 없어요';
        msg.classList.add('error');
      } else {
        msg.textContent = '잠시 후 다시 시도해주세요';
        msg.classList.add('error');
      }
    } catch {
      msg.textContent = '네트워크 오류';
      msg.classList.add('error');
    } finally {
      btn.disabled = false;
    }
  }
  function playInviteBurst(princess) {
    const burst = document.getElementById('inviteBurst');
    const core = document.getElementById('inviteBurstCore');
    const text = document.getElementById('inviteBurstText');
    if (!burst || !core || !text) return;
    if (princess && princess.avatarUrl) {
      core.innerHTML = `<img src="${princess.avatarUrl}" alt="">`;
    } else {
      core.textContent = '👑';
    }
    text.textContent = `${princess?.displayName || '새로운 공주'}님이 합류했어요 ✨`;
    [...burst.querySelectorAll('.ib-sparkle')].forEach(s => s.remove());
    const N = 10;
    for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) {
      const sp = document.createElement('span');
      sp.className = 'ib-sparkle';
      sp.textContent = ['✨','💖','🌸','⭐'][i % 4];
      const angle = (Math.PI * 2 * i) / N + Math.random() * 0.4;
      const dist = 80 + Math.random() * 60;
      sp.style.setProperty('--dx', `${Math.cos(angle) * dist}px`);
      sp.style.setProperty('--dy', `${Math.sin(angle) * dist}px`);
      sp.style.animationDelay = `${Math.random() * 0.15}s`;
      burst.appendChild(sp);
    }
    burst.classList.add('show');
    setTimeout(() => burst.classList.remove('show'), 1700);
  }

  function princessGradeEmoji(n) {
    if (n >= 30) return '👑';
    if (n >= 15) return '🌹';
    if (n >= 5) return '🌷';
    return '🌱';
  }

  function renderProfileGrid(cards) {
    const grid = document.getElementById('igProfileGrid');
    const empty = document.getElementById('igProfileEmpty');
    grid.innerHTML = '';
    if (!cards.length) { empty.classList.add('show'); return; }
    empty.classList.remove('show');
    cards.forEach(card => {
      const img = card.querySelector('.post-image');
      const text = card.querySelector('.post-content')?.textContent || '';
      const item = document.createElement('div');
      item.className = 'ig-grid-item';
      if (img && img.src) {
        item.innerHTML = `<img src="${img.src}" alt="">`;
      } else {
        item.innerHTML = `<div class="grid-text">${escHtml(text.slice(0, 50))}</div>`;
      }
      item.onclick = () => {
        goHome();
        setTimeout(() => card.scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth', block:'center'}), 50);
      };
      grid.appendChild(item);
    });
  }

  // ── My Profile ──
  let myProfile = loadProfile();

  function genIpCode() {
    const chars = 'ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ23456789';
    const block = () => Array.from({length:4}, () => chars[Math.floor(Math.random()*chars.length)]).join('');
    return `PRC-${block()}-${block()}`;
  }

  function loadProfile() {
    // 초기 동기 로드: localStorage 캐시(있으면) 또는 빈 값. 직후 syncProfileFromServer()가 서버 값으로 덮어씀.
    try {
      const raw = localStorage.getItem('princess_profile');
      if (raw) return JSON.parse(raw);
    } catch (e) {}
    return {
      username: 'princess_chae',
      avatarDataUrl: null,
      ipCode: '',
      mbti: '',
      birthDate: '',
      birthTime: ''
    };
  }

  async function syncProfileFromServer() {
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/me', { credentials: 'same-origin' });
      if (!res.ok) return;
      const json = await res.json();
      if (!json.ok || !json.user?.profile) return;
      const p = json.user.profile;
      myProfile = {
        username: p.username || 'princess_chae',
        name: p.princessName || '',
        bio: p.bio || '',
        avatarDataUrl: p.avatarUrl || null,
        ipCode: p.ipCode || '',
        mbti: p.mbti || '',
        birthDate: p.birthDate ? new Date(p.birthDate).toISOString().slice(0, 10) : '',
        birthTime: p.birthTime || ''
      };
      try { localStorage.setItem('princess_profile', JSON.stringify(myProfile)); } catch (e) {}
      try { renderMyProfile && renderMyProfile(); } catch (e) {}
      try { fillProfileForm && fillProfileForm(); } catch (e) {}
    } catch (e) { console.warn('[profile] sync failed', e); }
  }
  if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
    window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => { syncProfileFromServer(); });
  }

  async function saveProfile() {
    const username = document.getElementById('profileUsername').value.trim() || 'princess_chae';
    myProfile.username = username;
    myProfile.bio = document.getElementById('profileBio')?.value.trim() || '';
    myProfile.mbti = document.getElementById('profileMbti').value;
    myProfile.birthDate = document.getElementById('profileBirthDate').value;
    myProfile.birthTime = document.getElementById('profileBirthTime').value;
    if (pendingAvatar !== undefined) {
      myProfile.avatarDataUrl = pendingAvatar;
      pendingAvatar = undefined;
    }
    try { localStorage.setItem('princess_profile', JSON.stringify(myProfile)); } catch (e) {}
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/me', {
        method: 'PATCH',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        credentials: 'same-origin',
        body: JSON.stringify({
          username: myProfile.username || undefined,
          bio: myProfile.bio ?? undefined,
          mbti: myProfile.mbti || undefined,
          birthDate: myProfile.birthDate || undefined,
          birthTime: myProfile.birthTime || undefined,
          avatarUrl: myProfile.avatarDataUrl || undefined
        })
      });
      if (!res.ok) {
        let msg = '서버 저장 실패 ❌';
        try { const j = await res.json(); if (j?.error === 'UsernameTaken') msg = '이미 사용 중인 ID예요'; } catch {}
        showToast(msg);
        return;
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn('[profile] save failed', e);
      showToast('서버 저장 실패 ❌');
    }
    renderMyProfile();
    showToast('저장 완료 👑');
    goProfile();
  }

  function cancelProfile() {
    pendingAvatar = undefined;
    fillProfileForm();
    goProfile();
  }

  let pendingAvatar;
  function onAvatarPicked(e) {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = () => {
      pendingAvatar = reader.result;
      renderProfileAvatarPreview(pendingAvatar);
    };
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
  }

  function renderProfileAvatarPreview(src) {
    const content = document.getElementById('profileAvatarContent');
    if (src) {
      content.innerHTML = `<img src="${src}" alt="me">`;
    } else {
      content.textContent = '👸';
    }
  }

  function fillProfileForm() {
    document.getElementById('profileUsername').value = myProfile.username;
    const bioEl = document.getElementById('profileBio'); if (bioEl) bioEl.value = myProfile.bio || '';
    document.getElementById('profileMbti').value = myProfile.mbti || '';
    document.getElementById('profileBirthDate').value = myProfile.birthDate || '';
    document.getElementById('profileBirthTime').value = myProfile.birthTime || '';
    renderProfileAvatarPreview(myProfile.avatarDataUrl);
  }

  function avatarHtml(profile, size) {
    if (profile.avatarDataUrl) {
      return `<img src="${profile.avatarDataUrl}" alt="me" style="width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:cover;border-radius:50%;">`;
    }
    return '👸';
  }

  function renderMyProfile() {
    // write box
    const wa = document.getElementById('writeAvatar');
    wa.innerHTML = avatarHtml(myProfile);
    document.getElementById('writeName').textContent = myProfile.username;
    document.getElementById('writeSub').textContent = `오늘 하루를 기록해봐 🌷`;
    // story
    const sa = document.getElementById('myStoryAvatar');
    sa.innerHTML = avatarHtml(myProfile);
    // existing posts
    document.querySelectorAll('.post-card .post-username').forEach(el => {
      if (el.dataset.me === '1') el.textContent = myProfile.username;
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('.post-card .post-avatar').forEach(el => {
      if (el.dataset.me === '1') el.innerHTML = avatarHtml(myProfile);
    });
  }

  function copyIpCode() {
    navigator.clipboard?.writeText(myProfile.ipCode);
    showToast('IP 코드 복사됨 📋');
  }

  function showToast(msg) {
    const t = document.getElementById('toast');
    t.textContent = msg;
    t.classList.add('show');
    clearTimeout(showToast._t);
    showToast._t = setTimeout(() => t.classList.remove('show'), 1800);
  }

  // ── Post menu ──
  function togglePostMenu(e, postId) {
    e.stopPropagation();
    const menu = document.getElementById(`${postId}_menu`);
    if (!menu) return;
    document.querySelectorAll('.post-menu.open').forEach(m => { if (m !== menu) m.classList.remove('open'); });
    menu.classList.toggle('open');
  }
  document.addEventListener('click', () => {
    document.querySelectorAll('.post-menu.open').forEach(m => m.classList.remove('open'));
  });

  function deletePost(postId) {
    const card = document.getElementById(postId);
    if (card) card.remove();
    delete POST_TEXTS[postId];
    const feed = document.getElementById('dynamicFeed');
    if (feed && !feed.children.length) {
      document.getElementById('emptyHint').style.display = '';
    }
    persistState();
  }

  function pinPost(postId) {
    const card = document.getElementById(postId);
    if (!card) return;
    const feed = document.getElementById('dynamicFeed');
    const info = card.querySelector('.post-info');
    const existingLabel = info && info.querySelector('.pinned-label');
    if (card.classList.contains('pinned')) {
      card.classList.remove('pinned');
      if (existingLabel) existingLabel.remove();
    } else {
      card.classList.add('pinned');
      if (info && !existingLabel) {
        const label = document.createElement('span');
        label.className = 'pinned-label';
        label.textContent = '📌 고정됨';
        info.appendChild(label);
      }
      feed.insertBefore(card, feed.firstChild);
    }
    document.getElementById(`${postId}_menu`)?.classList.remove('open');
    persistState();
  }

  // Initial render
  renderMyProfile();
  restoreState();
  migrateExistingMyPosts();

  // Emoji quick insert
  document.querySelectorAll('.write-emojis span').forEach(span => {
    span.style.cursor = 'pointer';
    span.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const ta = document.getElementById('diaryInput');
      ta.value += span.textContent;
      ta.focus();
    });
  });

  // ── Phase 2: /api/princesses 동기화 ──
  // Next.js 하위 /app 라우트에서 서빙될 때만 동작. 정적 file:// 환경에서는 fetch 실패 → 무시.
  (async function syncPrincessesFromDB() {
    try {
      // 절대 경로로 호출 (index.html이 /app/ 하위에서 서빙되어도 /api/...로 정상 도달)
      const res = await fetch('/api/princesses', { credentials: 'same-origin' });
      if (!res.ok) return;
      const json = await res.json();
      if (!json?.ok || !Array.isArray(json.princesses)) return;
      let touched = 0;
      for (const p of json.princesses) {
        const id = p.id;
        // 1) MODERATION 덮어쓰기
        if (typeof MODERATION !== 'undefined' && MODERATION[id] && p.moderation) {
          Object.assign(MODERATION[id], {
            topics: p.moderation.topics || MODERATION[id].topics,
            warningLine: p.moderation.warningLine || MODERATION[id].warningLine,
            modalText: p.moderation.modalText || MODERATION[id].modalText,
            yesReply: p.moderation.yesReply || MODERATION[id].yesReply,
            noNotice: p.moderation.noNotice || MODERATION[id].noNotice,
            muteMs: typeof p.moderation.muteMs === 'number' ? p.moderation.muteMs : MODERATION[id].muteMs
          });
          touched++;
        }
        // 2) CHARACTERS systemPrompt 보강 (서버 데이터를 systemPrompt 뒤에 한 줄 추가)
        if (typeof CHARACTERS !== 'undefined' && Array.isArray(CHARACTERS)) {
          const c = CHARACTERS.find(x => x.id === id);
          if (c && p.speakingStyle && c.systemPrompt && !c._dbAugmented) {
            const extra = `\n[DB 보강] MBTI: ${p.mbti || ''} / 대표 한마디: ${p.representative?.quote || ''} / 자주쓰는 표현 예: ${(p.frequentPhrases || []).slice(0,3).join(' · ')}`;
            c.systemPrompt += extra;
            c._dbAugmented = true;
          }
        }
      }
      console.log('[princessgram] synced from /api/princesses, touched=', touched);
      window.__PRINCESSGRAM_DB_SYNCED__ = true;
    } catch (e) {
      // 정적 서빙(file://, GitHub Pages 등)에서는 정상적으로 실패 — 무시
      console.log('[princessgram] /api/princesses unavailable, using local defaults');
    }
  })();

  // ── 관리자 floating 버튼 (ADMIN_EMAILS 목록의 이메일로 로그인 시만 표시) ──
  (async function injectAdminLink() {
    var ADMIN_EMAILS = ['wontyme88@naver.com', 'dongsoonh@lannerinc.co.kr'];
    try {
      var res = await fetch('/api/me', { credentials: 'same-origin' });
      if (!res.ok) return;
      var j = await res.json();
      var email = j && j.user && j.user.email ? String(j.user.email).toLowerCase() : '';
      if (!email || ADMIN_EMAILS.indexOf(email) < 0) return;
      var a = document.createElement('a');
      a.href = '/admin/princesses';
      a.textContent = '⚙ 관리자';
      a.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;right:12px;bottom:12px;z-index:2147483647;background:#ec4899;color:#fff;padding:10px 14px;border-radius:999px;font-size:13px;font-weight:700;text-decoration:none;box-shadow:0 4px 14px rgba(236,72,153,0.45)';
      document.body.appendChild(a);
    } catch (e) {}
  })();

  // ── Phase 3: 관리자 작성 공주 게시글을 DB에서 가져와 CHARACTER_PROFILES에 prepend ──
  (async function syncPrincessPostsFromDB() {
    if (typeof CHARACTER_PROFILES === 'undefined') return;
    const ids = Object.keys(CHARACTER_PROFILES);
    await Promise.all(ids.map(async (id) => {
      try {
        const res = await fetch(`/api/princesses/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/posts?limit=30`, { credentials: 'same-origin' });
        if (!res.ok) return;
        const json = await res.json();
        if (!json?.ok || !Array.isArray(json.items) || !json.items.length) return;
        const profile = CHARACTER_PROFILES[id];
        if (!profile) return;
        // 최신순(items[0] 최신) → 가장 앞에 표시되도록 역순으로 unshift
        const dbPosts = json.items.slice().reverse().map(item => {
          const urls = Array.isArray(item.imageUrls) && item.imageUrls.length
            ? item.imageUrls
            : (item.imageUrl ? [item.imageUrl] : []);
          return {
            emoji: item.emoji || '✨',
            caption: item.text || '',
            image: urls[0] || null,
            images: urls.length > 1 ? urls : undefined,
            comments: [], // 초기 렌더에서는 비워두기
            id: item.id,
            __db: true,
            __dbComments: Array.isArray(item.comments) ? item.comments : []
          };
        });
        // DB 게시글이 있으면 하드코딩 게시글은 무시 (중복 방지)
        profile.posts = dbPosts;
      } catch (e) { /* ignore */ }
    }));
    console.log('[princessgram] DB princess posts synced');
    // 이미 열린 캐릭터 프로필이 있다면 재렌더 (sync가 사용자 클릭보다 늦게 끝난 경우)
    try {
      const openProfile = document.querySelector('.profile-page.character-profile.open, #characterProfilePage.open');
      const charProfileGrid = document.querySelector('[id^="charProfileGrid_"]');
      if (charProfileGrid) {
        const m = charProfileGrid.id.match(/^charProfileGrid_(.+)$/);
        if (m && typeof goCharacterProfile === 'function') {
          goCharacterProfile(m[1]);
        }
      }
    } catch (e) {}
  })();

  // 현재 로그인된 사용자 닉네임을 헤더에 적용 (Next.js /app 환경)
  (async function syncCurrentUser() {
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/me', { credentials: 'same-origin' });
      if (!res.ok) return;
      const json = await res.json();
      const u = json?.user;
      if (!u) return;
      const headerName = document.getElementById('igProfileUsername');
      if (headerName && u.profile?.username) headerName.textContent = u.profile.username;
      window.__PRINCESSGRAM_USER__ = u;
    } catch {}
  })();

  // ── Phase 2: 친밀도 이벤트 핑 ──
  // 사용자 액션마다 /api/intimacy/event 로 가벼운 핑.
  // 로그인 안 됐거나 정적 환경이면 조용히 무시.
  window.princessGramPing = async function(princessId, action, delta) {
    if (!princessId || !action) return;
    try {
      await fetch('/api/intimacy/event', {
        method: 'POST',
        credentials: 'same-origin',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ princessId, action, ...(typeof delta === 'number' ? { delta } : {}) })
      });
    } catch {}
  };

  // 좋아요·DM·댓글·답글·모더레이션 흐름에 자동 후킹
  // 캐릭터 게시글 댓글 id 매핑: localKey "charId|idx" → 최신 dbCommentId
  window.__charCommentMap = window.__charCommentMap || {};
  (function hookIntimacy() {
    // 1) 캐릭터 게시글 좋아요 — 친밀도 + DB 토글
    if (typeof toggleCharPostLike === 'function') {
      const orig = toggleCharPostLike;
      window.toggleCharPostLike = function(btn, charId, idx) {
        const wasLiked = btn.textContent === '❤️';
        const r = orig.apply(this, arguments);
        if (!wasLiked) window.princessGramPing(charId, 'like');
        // DB toggle
        fetch(`/api/char-posts/${encodeURIComponent(charId)}/${idx}/like`, {
          method: 'POST',
          credentials: 'same-origin'
        }).catch(() => {});
        return r;
      };
    }
    // 2) DM 전송 (사용자→공주)
    if (typeof sendUserDM === 'function') {
      const orig = sendUserDM;
      window.sendUserDM = async function() {
        const target = (typeof _openDMCharId !== 'undefined') ? _openDMCharId : null;
        const r = await orig.apply(this, arguments);
        if (target) window.princessGramPing(target, 'dm_send');
        return r;
      };
    }
    // 3) 캐릭터 게시글에 사용자 댓글 — 친밀도 + DB
    if (typeof submitCharPostComment === 'function') {
      const orig = submitCharPostComment;
      window.submitCharPostComment = async function(charId, idx) {
        const safeKey = (charId+'-'+idx).replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g,'_');
        const input = document.getElementById(`cpc_input_${safeKey}`);
        const text = input ? input.value.trim() : '';
        const r = await orig.apply(this, arguments);
        window.princessGramPing(charId, 'comment');
        if (text) {
          try {
            const res = await fetch(`/api/char-posts/${encodeURIComponent(charId)}/${idx}/comments`, {
              method: 'POST',
              credentials: 'same-origin',
              headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
              body: JSON.stringify({ text })
            });
            if (res.ok) {
              const j = await res.json();
              if (j?.comment?.id) window.__charCommentMap[`${charId}|${idx}`] = j.comment.id;
            }
          } catch {}
        }
        return r;
      };
    }
    // 4) 공주가 단 댓글에 사용자 답글
    if (typeof submitReply === 'function') {
      const orig = submitReply;
      window.submitReply = function(postId, cid, charId) {
        const r = orig.apply(this, arguments);
        window.princessGramPing(charId, 'reply');
        return r;
      };
    }
    // 5) 모더레이션 모달 — 예/아니오
    if (typeof showWarningDialog === 'function') {
      const orig = showWarningDialog;
      window.showWarningDialog = function(opts) {
        const wrapped = {
          ...opts,
          onYes: () => {
            if (opts?.char?.id) window.princessGramPing(opts.char.id, 'moderation_yes');
            opts?.onYes && opts.onYes();
          },
          onNo: () => {
            if (opts?.char?.id) window.princessGramPing(opts.char.id, 'moderation_no');
            opts?.onNo && opts.onNo();
          }
        };
        return orig(wrapped);
      };
    }
    // 6) 부적절 발언 감지 시
    if (typeof triggerModeration === 'function') {
      const orig = triggerModeration;
      window.triggerModeration = function(charId, topic, surface, ctx) {
        window.princessGramPing(charId, 'inappropriate');
        return orig(charId, topic, surface, ctx);
      };
    }
    console.log('[princessgram] intimacy hooks installed');
  })();

  // ── Phase 4.2: 이미지 업로드 헬퍼 (클라 리사이즈 → /api/upload) ──
  window.PrincessUpload = (function() {
    async function fileToResizedDataUrl(file, maxSide, quality) {
      maxSide = maxSide || 1024;
      quality = quality || 0.8;
      const bitmap = await createImageBitmap(file);
      let w = bitmap.width, h = bitmap.height;
      if (Math.max(w, h) > maxSide) {
        const r = maxSide / Math.max(w, h);
        w = Math.round(w * r); h = Math.round(h * r);
      }
      const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
      canvas.width = w; canvas.height = h;
      const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
      ctx.drawImage(bitmap, 0, 0, w, h);
      return canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg', quality);
    }
    async function uploadFile(file) {
      try {
        const dataUrl = await fileToResizedDataUrl(file, 1024, 0.8);
        const res = await fetch('/api/upload', {
          method: 'POST',
          credentials: 'same-origin',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({ dataUrl })
        });
        if (!res.ok) return null;
        const j = await res.json();
        return j.url || null;
      } catch (e) {
        console.warn('[upload] failed', e);
        return null;
      }
    }
    return { fileToResizedDataUrl, uploadFile };
  })();

  // ── Phase 5.3: persona summary 자동 생성 ──
  // 일정 주기(현재 일기 5편마다)로 사용자 일기들을 요약해 메모리에 저장.
  // 사용자 OpenAI 키를 그대로 사용 (옵션 A). 키 없으면 패스.
  window.maybeGeneratePersonaSummary = async function() {
    try {
      const key = (localStorage.getItem('openai_api_key') || '').trim();
      if (!key || !key.startsWith('sk-')) return;

      const lastAt = parseInt(localStorage.getItem('persona_summary_last') || '0', 10);
      // 최소 6시간 간격
      if (Date.now() - lastAt < 6 * 60 * 60_000) return;

      // 내 게시글 가져오기
      const res = await fetch('/api/posts?limit=20', { credentials: 'same-origin' });
      if (!res.ok) return;
      const j = await res.json();
      const posts = (j.posts || []);
      if (posts.length < 5) return;          // 일기 5편 미만이면 스킵

      const sampleText = posts.slice(0, 15)
        .map(p => `- ${p.text.slice(0, 240)}`)
        .join('\n');

      const sys = '너는 사용자의 일기들을 읽고 그 사람의 성향·관심사·말투를 간결한 한국어로 요약하는 분석가야. 출력은 자유 문장 6줄 이하. 따옴표나 마크다운 없음.';
      const usr = `다음은 같은 사용자가 최근에 쓴 일기 ${posts.length}편이야:\n${sampleText}\n\n위 일기들에서 보이는 사용자의 성향·자주 다루는 주제·기분 패턴·말투·관심사를 6줄 이내로 요약해줘. 미래의 AI 친구가 이 사람을 더 잘 이해할 수 있게.`;

      // OpenAI chat (사용자 키)
      const cres = await fetch('https://api.openai.com/v1/chat/completions', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Authorization': `Bearer ${key}` },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          model: 'gpt-4o-mini',
          messages: [{ role: 'system', content: sys }, { role: 'user', content: usr }],
          temperature: 0.4,
          max_tokens: 240
        })
      });
      if (!cres.ok) return;
      const cd = await cres.json();
      const summary = (cd.choices?.[0]?.message?.content || '').trim();
      if (!summary || summary.length < 20) return;

      // 메모리에 저장 (princessId 없음 = 공통)
      await window.PrincessMemory.save({ kind: 'persona_summary', text: summary, meta: { source_count: posts.length } });
      localStorage.setItem('persona_summary_last', String(Date.now()));
      console.log('[princessgram] persona summary saved');
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn('[princessgram] persona summary failed', e);
    }
  };
  // 페이지 로드 후 살짝 지연시켜 실행
  setTimeout(() => { try { window.maybeGeneratePersonaSummary(); } catch {} }, 8000);

  // ── Phase 3: pgvector 메모리 (옵션 A — 사용자 본인 OpenAI 키 사용) ──
  window.PrincessMemory = (function() {
    async function getOpenAIKey() {
      return (localStorage.getItem('openai_api_key') || '').trim();
    }
    async function embed(text) {
      const key = await getOpenAIKey();
      if (!key || !key.startsWith('sk-')) return null;
      try {
        const res = await fetch('https://api.openai.com/v1/embeddings', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Authorization': `Bearer ${key}` },
          body: JSON.stringify({ model: 'text-embedding-3-small', input: text.slice(0, 8000) })
        });
        if (!res.ok) return null;
        const data = await res.json();
        return data?.data?.[0]?.embedding || null;
      } catch {
        return null;
      }
    }
    async function save({ princessId, kind, text, meta }) {
      if (!text || !text.trim()) return null;
      const embedding = await embed(text);
      if (!embedding) return null;
      try {
        const res = await fetch('/api/memory/save', {
          method: 'POST',
          credentials: 'same-origin',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({ princessId: princessId || null, kind, text: text.slice(0, 8000), embedding, meta: meta || {} })
        });
        return res.ok ? (await res.json()).id : null;
      } catch {
        return null;
      }
    }
    async function search({ princessId, kind, text, limit }) {
      if (!text || !text.trim()) return [];
      const embedding = await embed(text);
      if (!embedding) return [];
      try {
        const res = await fetch('/api/memory/search', {
          method: 'POST',
          credentials: 'same-origin',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            ...(princessId ? { princessId } : {}),
            ...(kind ? { kind } : {}),
            embedding,
            limit: limit || 5
          })
        });
        if (!res.ok) return [];
        const data = await res.json();
        return data.hits || [];
      } catch {
        return [];
      }
    }
    function snippetsToPromptBlock(hits) {
      if (!hits || !hits.length) return '';
      const lines = hits.slice(0, 5).map((h, i) => {
        const who = h.princessId ? `(${h.princessId})` : '(공통)';
        return `${i + 1}. ${who} ${h.kind}: ${h.text.slice(0, 240)}`;
      });
      return `\n[관련 기억 — 사용자의 과거 일기/대화에서 가장 관련 있는 항목. 자연스럽게 반영만 하고 직접 인용 금지]\n${lines.join('\n')}`;
    }
    return { embed, save, search, snippetsToPromptBlock };
  })();

  // 친밀도 캐시 (페이지당 1회 fetch, 5분 TTL)
  window.__intimacyCache = { at: 0, byPrincess: {} };
  async function getIntimacyMap() {
    const now = Date.now();
    if (now - window.__intimacyCache.at < 5 * 60_000) return window.__intimacyCache.byPrincess;
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/intimacy/me', { credentials: 'same-origin' });
      if (!res.ok) return {};
      const j = await res.json();
      const map = {};
      for (const r of (j.relations || [])) map[r.princessId] = r.intimacy;
      window.__intimacyCache = { at: now, byPrincess: map };
      return map;
    } catch { return {}; }
  }
  function intimacyToneLine(score) {
    if (score <= 0) return null;
    if (score < 20) return '[친밀도] 안면을 텄지만 아직 조심스러운 사이. 살짝만 더 자연스럽게.';
    if (score < 50) return '[친밀도] 친구. 평소 톤 그대로 친근하게.';
    if (score < 100) return '[친밀도] 친한 친구. 장난과 농담을 조금 더 섞고, 사적인 이야기도 살짝 꺼낼 만한 사이.';
    if (score < 200) return '[친밀도] 베프. 매우 편하게, 줄임말과 본심을 자유롭게. 이전 대화를 기억하는 느낌으로.';
    return '[친밀도] 절친. 가장 가까운 친구처럼. 짧고 솔직하게, 거의 가족 같은 톤.';
  }

  // aiChat을 패치 — 메모리 검색 + 친밀도 톤 라인 주입
  (function patchAiChatWithMemoryAndIntimacy() {
    if (typeof aiChat !== 'function') return;
    const orig = aiChat;
    window.aiChat = async function(opts) {
      try {
        const userText = (opts && opts.user) ? String(opts.user) : '';
        const sys = (opts && opts.system) ? String(opts.system) : '';
        let pid = null;
        const m = sys.match(/'(ruby_|lily_princess|sora_diary|momo__o|pearl_diary)'/);
        if (m) pid = m[1];

        // 1) 친밀도 톤
        if (pid) {
          const map = await getIntimacyMap();
          const tone = intimacyToneLine(map[pid] || 0);
          if (tone) opts = { ...opts, system: (opts.system || '') + '\n' + tone };
        }
        // 2) 메모리 회상
        if (userText && userText.length > 4) {
          const hits = await window.PrincessMemory.search({ princessId: pid, text: userText.slice(0, 1000), limit: 4 });
          if (hits && hits.length) {
            opts = { ...opts, system: (opts.system || '') + window.PrincessMemory.snippetsToPromptBlock(hits) };
          }
        }
      } catch {}
      return orig(opts);
    };
    console.log('[princessgram] aiChat patched with memory + intimacy tone');
  })();

  // localPostId → dbPostId 매핑 (백필된 글이나 갓 만든 글이나 같은 매핑 테이블)
  window.__postIdMap = window.__postIdMap || {};

  // 일기 발행 시: 메모리 저장 + DB write-through (매핑 기록)
  (function hookDiarySave() {
    if (typeof publishPost !== 'function') return;
    const orig = publishPost;
    window.publishPost = async function() {
      const ta = document.getElementById('diaryInput');
      const text = ta ? ta.value.trim() : '';
      const img = (typeof pendingPostImage !== 'undefined') ? pendingPostImage : null;
      const beforeCount = (typeof postCount !== 'undefined') ? postCount : 0;
      const r = await orig.apply(this, arguments);
      const localPostId = 'post_' + (typeof postCount !== 'undefined' ? postCount : (beforeCount + 1));
      if (text) {
        window.PrincessMemory.save({ kind: 'diary', text });
        try {
          // 이미지는 데이터 URL이거나 http URL일 수 있음. http면 그대로, 데이터URL이면 길이 제한 (~2MB Postgres TEXT 안전)
          let imageUrl = null;
          if (img && typeof img === 'string') {
            if (img.startsWith('http')) imageUrl = img;
            else if (img.startsWith('data:image') && img.length < 2_000_000) imageUrl = img;
          }
          const res = await fetch('/api/posts', {
            method: 'POST',
            credentials: 'same-origin',
            headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
            body: JSON.stringify({ text, imageUrl })
          });
          if (res.ok) {
            const j = await res.json();
            if (j?.post?.id) {
              window.__postIdMap[localPostId] = j.post.id;
              window.__lastDbPostId = j.post.id;
            }
          }
        } catch {}
      }
      return r;
    };
  })();

  // 공주 자동 댓글도 DB로 저장: addComment 후킹
  (function hookPrincessAutoComment() {
    if (typeof addComment !== 'function') return;
    const orig = addComment;
    window.addComment = function(postId, char, text, elapsedSec) {
      const r = orig.apply(this, arguments);
      const dbPostId = window.__postIdMap[postId];
      if (dbPostId && char && char.id && text) {
        fetch(`/api/posts/${encodeURIComponent(dbPostId)}/comments`, {
          method: 'POST',
          credentials: 'same-origin',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({ princessId: char.id, text })
        }).catch(() => {});
      }
      return r;
    };
  })();

  // DM 전송 시: 메모리 저장 + DB write-through
  (function hookDmSave() {
    if (typeof sendUserDM !== 'function') return;
    const orig = sendUserDM;
    window.sendUserDM = async function() {
      const input = document.getElementById('dmInput');
      const text = input ? input.value.trim() : '';
      const target = (typeof _openDMCharId !== 'undefined') ? _openDMCharId : null;
      const r = await orig.apply(this, arguments);
      if (text && target) {
        window.PrincessMemory.save({ kind: 'dm_user', text, princessId: target });
        try {
          await fetch(`/api/dm/${encodeURIComponent(target)}`, {
            method: 'POST',
            credentials: 'same-origin',
            headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
            body: JSON.stringify({ fromMe: true, text })
          });
        } catch {}
      }
      return r;
    };
  })();

  // 내 일기에 사용자가 단 댓글도 DB로
  (function hookMyPostComment() {
    if (typeof submitMyPostComment !== 'function') return;
    const orig = submitMyPostComment;
    window.submitMyPostComment = function(postId) {
      const inp = document.getElementById(`${postId}_userCommentInput`);
      const text = inp ? inp.value.trim() : '';
      const r = orig.apply(this, arguments);
      const dbId = (window.__postIdMap && window.__postIdMap[postId]) || window.__lastDbPostId;
      if (text && dbId) {
        fetch(`/api/posts/${encodeURIComponent(dbId)}/comments`, {
          method: 'POST',
          credentials: 'same-origin',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({ text, princessId: null })
        }).catch(() => {});
      }
      return r;
    };
  })();

  // ── Phase 4.1: 신규 기기 백필 (DB → 로컬 UI/스토리지) ──
  // /app 환경에서만, 그리고 현재 피드가 비어있을 때만 동작.
  (async function backfillFromServer() {
    try {
      const feed = document.getElementById('dynamicFeed');
      if (!feed) return;
      const hasLocalPosts = feed.querySelectorAll('.post-card').length > 0;
      // 게시글 백필
      if (!hasLocalPosts) {
        const res = await fetch('/api/posts?limit=50', { credentials: 'same-origin' });
        if (res.ok) {
          const json = await res.json();
          const posts = (json.posts || []).slice().reverse(); // 오래된 것부터 만들고 prepend
          let count = 0;
          for (const p of posts) {
            count++;
            const postId = 'post_' + (++postCount);
            POST_TEXTS[postId] = p.text;
            window.__postIdMap = window.__postIdMap || {};
            window.__postIdMap[postId] = p.id;
            const card = document.createElement('div');
            card.className = 'post-card post-border';
            card.id = postId;
            const mediaHtml = p.imageUrl
              ? `<img class="post-image" src="${p.imageUrl}" alt="">`
              : '';
            const created = new Date(p.createdAt).toLocaleString('ko-KR');
            card.innerHTML = `
              <div class="post-top">
                <div class="post-avatar" data-me="1">${avatarHtml(myProfile)}</div>
                <div class="post-info">
                  <div class="post-username" data-me="1">${escHtml(myProfile.username)}</div>
                  <div class="post-time">${created}</div>
                </div>
                <button class="post-menu-btn" onclick="togglePostMenu(event, '${postId}')">···</button>
                <div class="post-menu" id="${postId}_menu">
                  <button class="danger" onclick="deletePost('${postId}')">🗑 삭제</button>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div class="post-content">${escHtml(p.text)}</div>
              ${mediaHtml}
              <div class="post-actions">
                <button class="action-btn" onclick="toggleLike(this, '${postId}')">🤍</button>
                <button class="action-btn" onclick="focusMyPostComment('${postId}')">💬</button>
              </div>
              <div class="likes-count" id="${postId}_likes">좋아요 0개</div>
              <div class="comments" id="${postId}_comments"></div>
              <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;padding:10px 16px;border-top:1px solid #f3e3ec;align-items:center;">
                <div class="comment-avatar" style="width:28px;height:28px;">${avatarHtml(myProfile)}</div>
                <input type="text" id="${postId}_userCommentInput"
                  placeholder="댓글 달기..."
                  onkeydown="if(event.key==='Enter'&&!event.isComposing&&event.keyCode!==229)submitMyPostComment('${postId}')"
                  style="flex:1;border:none;outline:none;background:transparent;font-size:13px;color:#333;">
                <button onclick="submitMyPostComment('${postId}')"
                  class="comment-send-btn" aria-label="게시"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M12 19V5M5 12l7-7 7 7"/></svg></button>
              </div>
            `;
            feed.insertBefore(card, feed.firstChild);
            // 댓글 백필
            const cmtBox = card.querySelector('.comments');
            for (const c of (p.comments || [])) {
              const cid = `cmt_${++_commentSeq}`;
              const el = document.createElement('div');
              el.className = 'comment';
              el.id = cid;
              const nameHtml = c.princessId ? c.princessId : escHtml(myProfile.username);
              const charObj = c.princessId && typeof CHARACTERS !== 'undefined'
                ? CHARACTERS.find(x => x.id === c.princessId) : null;
              const avatarBlock = charObj
                ? `<div class="comment-avatar"><img src="${charObj.img}" alt="${c.princessId}" onerror="this.replaceWith(Object.assign(document.createElement('span'),{textContent:'${charObj.emoji}'}))"></div>`
                : `<div class="comment-avatar">${avatarHtml(myProfile)}</div>`;
              const replyBlock = charObj ? `
                <button class="reply-btn" onclick="openReplyInput('${postId}','${cid}','${charObj.id}')">답글 달기</button>
                <div class="reply-input-wrap" id="${cid}_replyWrap">
                  <input type="text" id="${cid}_replyInput" placeholder="@${charObj.id} 에게 답글..." onkeydown="if(event.key==='Enter'&&!event.isComposing&&event.keyCode!==229)submitReply('${postId}','${cid}','${charObj.id}')">
                  <button onclick="submitReply('${postId}','${cid}','${charObj.id}')">등록</button>
                </div>
                <div class="replies" id="${cid}_replies"></div>
              ` : '';
              el.innerHTML = `
                ${avatarBlock}
                <div class="comment-body">
                  <span class="comment-name">${nameHtml} </span>
                  <span class="comment-text">${escHtml(c.text)}</span>
                  <div class="comment-time">${new Date(c.createdAt).toLocaleString('ko-KR')}<span class="comment-delete" onclick="removeClosestComment(this)">삭제</span></div>
                  ${replyBlock}
                </div>
              `;
              cmtBox.appendChild(el);
            }
          }
          if (count > 0) {
            const hint = document.getElementById('emptyHint');
            if (hint) hint.style.display = 'none';
            persistState();
            console.log('[princessgram] backfilled', count, 'posts from DB');
          }
        }
      }
      // DM 백필
      if (typeof DM_THREADS !== 'undefined' && Object.keys(DM_THREADS).length === 0) {
        const tres = await fetch('/api/dm', { credentials: 'same-origin' });
        if (tres.ok) {
          const tjson = await tres.json();
          for (const t of (tjson.threads || [])) {
            const mres = await fetch(`/api/dm/${encodeURIComponent(t.princessId)}`, { credentials: 'same-origin' });
            if (!mres.ok) continue;
            const mjson = await mres.json();
            DM_THREADS[t.princessId] = {
              messages: (mjson.messages || []).map(m => ({
                from: m.fromMe ? 'me' : 'them',
                text: m.text,
                ts: new Date(m.createdAt).getTime()
              })),
              unread: t.unread || 0
            };
          }
          if (Object.keys(DM_THREADS).length > 0) {
            try { renderDMList(); updateDMBadge(); persistState(); } catch {}
            console.log('[princessgram] backfilled', Object.keys(DM_THREADS).length, 'DM threads');
          }
        }
      }
    } catch (e) {
      // 비로그인/정적 환경에서는 조용히 패스
    }
  })();

  // 메인피드 공주 카드의 댓글 입력 처리
  window.submitFeedCharComment = function(postId) {
    const input = document.getElementById(`feedCharPost_${postId}_input`);
    if (!input) return;
    const text = input.value.trim();
    if (!text) return;
    const box = document.getElementById(`feedCharPost_${postId}_comments`);
    if (!box) return;
    const el = document.createElement('div');
    el.className = 'comment';
    el.style.cssText = 'opacity:0;transform:translateY(6px);transition:opacity .35s ease,transform .35s ease;';
    el.innerHTML = `
      <div class="comment-avatar">${avatarHtml(myProfile)}</div>
      <div class="comment-body">
        <span class="comment-name">${escHtml(myProfile.username)}</span>
        <span class="comment-text">${escHtml(text)}</span>
        <div class="comment-time">방금<span class="comment-delete" onclick="removeClosestComment(this)">삭제</span></div>
      </div>
    `;
    box.appendChild(el);
    requestAnimationFrame(() => { el.style.opacity = '1'; el.style.transform = 'translateY(0)'; });
    input.value = '';
  };

  // ── 메인피드에 공주 게시글 통합 (인스타그램 스타일) ──
  (function setupPrincessFeedIntegration() {
    const RENDERED = new Set();
    let lastSyncAt = 0;
    let inFlight = false;

    function isVideoUrl(u) {
      return /^data:video\//.test(u) || /\.(mp4|webm|mov)(\?|$)/i.test(u);
    }

    function buildPrincessCard(charId, post, animateComments) {
      const prof = (typeof CHARACTER_PROFILES !== 'undefined') ? CHARACTER_PROFILES[charId] : null;
      const avatarSrc = prof?.img || '';
      const urls = Array.isArray(post.imageUrls) && post.imageUrls.length
        ? post.imageUrls
        : (post.imageUrl ? [post.imageUrl] : []);
      let mediaHtml = '';
      if (urls.length > 1) {
        mediaHtml = `<div class="post-carousel" style="position:relative;width:100%;aspect-ratio:1/1;overflow:hidden;">
          <div class="carousel-track" style="display:flex;width:100%;height:100%;transition:transform 0.3s ease;transform:translateX(0%);">
            ${urls.map(src => isVideoUrl(src)
              ? `<video src="${src}" controls playsinline style="width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:cover;flex-shrink:0;display:block;"></video>`
              : `<img src="${src}" alt="" style="width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:cover;flex-shrink:0;display:block;">`).join('')}
          </div>
          <button onclick="carouselMove(this,-1,${urls.length})" style="position:absolute;left:8px;top:50%;transform:translateY(-50%);background:rgba(255,255,255,0.85);border:none;width:32px;height:32px;border-radius:50%;font-size:18px;cursor:pointer;box-shadow:0 2px 6px rgba(0,0,0,0.2);">‹</button>
          <button onclick="carouselMove(this,1,${urls.length})" style="position:absolute;right:8px;top:50%;transform:translateY(-50%);background:rgba(255,255,255,0.85);border:none;width:32px;height:32px;border-radius:50%;font-size:18px;cursor:pointer;box-shadow:0 2px 6px rgba(0,0,0,0.2);">›</button>
        </div>`;
      } else if (urls.length === 1) {
        const src = urls[0];
        mediaHtml = isVideoUrl(src)
          ? `<video class="post-image" src="${src}" controls playsinline style="width:100%;aspect-ratio:1/1;object-fit:cover;display:block;background:#000;"></video>`
          : `<img class="post-image" src="${src}" alt="" style="width:100%;aspect-ratio:1/1;object-fit:cover;display:block;">`;
      }
      const card = document.createElement('div');
      card.className = 'post-card post-border';
      card.id = `feedCharPost_${post.id}`;
      card.dataset.postId = String(post.id);
      card.dataset.createdMs = String(new Date(post.createdAt).getTime() || 0);
      const timeText = new Date(post.createdAt).toLocaleString('ko-KR');
      card.innerHTML = `
        <div class="post-top">
          <div class="post-avatar"><img src="${avatarSrc}" alt="${charId}" style="width:100%;height:100%;border-radius:50%;object-fit:cover;"></div>
          <div class="post-info">
            <div class="post-username">${charId}</div>
            <div class="post-time">${timeText}</div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="post-content">${post.emoji ? `<span style="margin-right:6px;">${post.emoji}</span>` : ''}${escHtml(post.text || '')}</div>
        ${mediaHtml}
        <div class="post-actions">
          <button class="action-btn" onclick="toggleLikeByPostId(this, '${post.id}')">${isLikedByPostId(post.id) ? '❤️' : '🤍'}</button>
          <button class="action-btn" onclick="document.getElementById('feedCharPost_${post.id}_input')?.focus()">💬</button>
        </div>
        <div class="likes-count">좋아요 ${getLikeCountByPostId(post.id)}개</div>
        <div class="comments" id="feedCharPost_${post.id}_comments"></div>
        <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;padding:10px 16px;border-top:1px solid #f3e3ec;align-items:center;">
          <div class="comment-avatar" style="width:28px;height:28px;">${avatarHtml(myProfile)}</div>
          <input type="text" id="feedCharPost_${post.id}_input"
            placeholder="댓글 달기..."
            onkeydown="if(event.key==='Enter'&&!event.isComposing&&event.keyCode!==229)submitFeedCharComment('${post.id}')"
            style="flex:1;border:none;outline:none;background:transparent;font-size:13px;color:#333;">
          <button onclick="submitFeedCharComment('${post.id}')"
            class="comment-send-btn" aria-label="게시"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M12 19V5M5 12l7-7 7 7"/></svg></button>
        </div>
      `;
      // 저장된 공주 댓글
      const cmts = Array.isArray(post.comments) ? post.comments : [];
      const cmtBox = card.querySelector('.comments');
      // 이미 있던 게시글: 댓글 즉시 표시. 새로 들어온 게시글: 입력중 → staggered fade-in
      if (!animateComments) {
        cmts.forEach((c) => {
          const cprof = (typeof CHARACTER_PROFILES !== 'undefined') ? CHARACTER_PROFILES[c.id] : null;
          if (!cprof) return;
          const el = document.createElement('div');
          el.className = 'comment';
          el.innerHTML = `
            <div class="comment-avatar"><img src="${cprof.img}" alt="${c.id}" style="width:100%;height:100%;border-radius:50%;object-fit:cover;"></div>
            <div class="comment-body">
              <span class="comment-name">${c.id}</span>
              <span class="comment-text">${escHtml(c.text)}</span>
              <div class="comment-time">방금</div>
            </div>
          `;
          cmtBox.appendChild(el);
        });
        return card;
      }
      const TYPING_MS = 1200;
      const GAP_MS = 1800;
      cmts.forEach((c, k) => {
        const baseDelay = 800 + k * GAP_MS;
        // 입력 중 indicator
        setTimeout(() => {
          if (!document.body.contains(card)) return;
          const cprof = (typeof CHARACTER_PROFILES !== 'undefined') ? CHARACTER_PROFILES[c.id] : null;
          if (!cprof) return;
          const typingId = `feedCharPost_${post.id}_typing_${k}`;
          const typing = document.createElement('div');
          typing.className = 'typing-indicator';
          typing.id = typingId;
          typing.innerHTML = `
            <div class="comment-avatar" style="width:28px;height:28px;"><img src="${cprof.img}" alt="${c.id}" style="width:100%;height:100%;border-radius:50%;object-fit:cover;"></div>
            <span style="font-size:12px;color:#bbb;">${c.id} 입력중</span>
            <div class="typing-dots"><span></span><span></span><span></span></div>
          `;
          cmtBox.appendChild(typing);
        }, baseDelay);
        // 실제 댓글로 교체
        setTimeout(() => {
          if (!document.body.contains(card)) return;
          const cprof = (typeof CHARACTER_PROFILES !== 'undefined') ? CHARACTER_PROFILES[c.id] : null;
          if (!cprof) return;
          const typing = document.getElementById(`feedCharPost_${post.id}_typing_${k}`);
          if (typing) typing.remove();
          const el = document.createElement('div');
          el.className = 'comment';
          el.style.cssText = 'opacity:0;transform:translateY(6px);transition:opacity .35s ease,transform .35s ease;';
          el.innerHTML = `
            <div class="comment-avatar"><img src="${cprof.img}" alt="${c.id}" style="width:100%;height:100%;border-radius:50%;object-fit:cover;"></div>
            <div class="comment-body">
              <span class="comment-name">${c.id}</span>
              <span class="comment-text">${escHtml(c.text)}</span>
              <div class="comment-time">방금</div>
            </div>
          `;
          cmtBox.appendChild(el);
          requestAnimationFrame(() => { el.style.opacity = '1'; el.style.transform = 'translateY(0)'; });
        }, baseDelay + TYPING_MS);
      });
      return card;
    }

    function insertSortedDesc(feed, card) {
      const cms = Number(card.dataset.createdMs) || 0;
      const existing = feed.querySelectorAll('.post-card');
      for (const ex of existing) {
        const em = Number(ex.dataset.createdMs) || (() => {
          const t = ex.querySelector('.post-time')?.textContent || '';
          const d = new Date(t).getTime();
          return isNaN(d) ? 0 : d;
        })();
        if (!ex.dataset.createdMs && em) ex.dataset.createdMs = String(em);
        if (cms > em) { feed.insertBefore(card, ex); return; }
      }
      feed.appendChild(card);
    }

    async function fetchPrincessPosts() {
      try {
        const pres = await fetch('/api/princesses', { credentials: 'same-origin' });
        if (!pres.ok) return [];
        const pjson = await pres.json();
        const ids = (pjson.princesses || []).map(p => p.id);
        const all = await Promise.all(ids.map(async (id) => {
          try {
            const r = await fetch(`/api/princesses/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/posts?limit=20`, { credentials: 'same-origin' });
            if (!r.ok) return [];
            const j = await r.json();
            return (j.items || []).map(it => ({ ...it, charId: id }));
          } catch { return []; }
        }));
        return all.flat();
      } catch { return []; }
    }

    function addIfNew(feed, it, animateComments) {
      const cardId = `feedCharPost_${it.id}`;
      const existing = document.getElementById(cardId) || feed.querySelector(`[data-post-id="${it.id}"]`);
      if (RENDERED.has(it.id) || existing) {
        RENDERED.add(it.id);
        if (existing && !existing.id) existing.id = cardId;
        return false;
      }
      RENDERED.add(it.id);
      const card = buildPrincessCard(it.charId, it, animateComments);
      insertSortedDesc(feed, card);
      const ts = new Date(it.createdAt).getTime();
      if (ts > lastSyncAt) lastSyncAt = ts;
      return true;
    }

    async function loadInitial() {
      if (inFlight) return;
      inFlight = true;
      try {
        const feed = document.getElementById('dynamicFeed');
        if (!feed) return;
        // 옛 잔재(다른 IIFE 실행, 복원된 카드 등) 전부 제거 후 fresh 렌더
        feed.querySelectorAll('[id^="feedCharPost_"], [data-post-id]').forEach(el => el.remove());
        RENDERED.clear();
        const items = await fetchPrincessPosts();
        items.sort((a,b) => new Date(b.createdAt) - new Date(a.createdAt));
        for (const it of items) addIfNew(feed, it, false); // 초기 로드: 댓글 즉시 표시
        const hint = document.getElementById('emptyHint');
        if (hint && feed.querySelectorAll('.post-card').length > 0) hint.style.display = 'none';
      } finally { inFlight = false; }
    }

    async function pollNew() {
      if (document.hidden || inFlight) return;
      inFlight = true;
      try {
        const feed = document.getElementById('dynamicFeed');
        if (!feed) return;
        const items = await fetchPrincessPosts();
        const fresh = items.filter(it => !RENDERED.has(it.id) && !document.getElementById(`feedCharPost_${it.id}`));
        fresh.sort((a,b) => new Date(a.createdAt) - new Date(b.createdAt));
        let added = 0;
        for (const it of fresh) if (addIfNew(feed, it, true)) added++; // 폴링으로 들어온 신규: 입력중 → staggered
        if (added) console.log('[princessgram] feed: +', added, 'new princess posts');
      } finally { inFlight = false; }
    }

    // 초기 로드 (기존 backfillFromServer 이후 200ms 뒤 — 유저 게시글 카드들이 먼저 자리잡도록)
    setTimeout(loadInitial, 300);
    // 60초 폴링
    setInterval(pollNew, 60_000);
    // 탭 다시 활성되면 즉시 한 번
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => { if (!document.hidden) pollNew(); });
  })();

  // ── 프로필 아바타 클릭 → 해당 프로필로 이동 (메신저/DM 화면 제외) ──
  (function setupAvatarNavigation() {
    document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      const el = e.target.closest('.post-avatar, .comment-avatar');
      if (!el) return;
      // 메신저/DM 영역은 제외
      if (el.closest('.dm-chat, .messenger-page')) return;
      // 본인 아바타: data-me 또는 캐릭터 alt가 없음
      const charImg = el.querySelector('img[alt]');
      const altId = charImg?.getAttribute('alt') || '';
      const isMine = el.dataset?.me === '1' ||
        !(typeof CHARACTERS !== 'undefined' && CHARACTERS.some(c => c.id === altId));
      e.stopPropagation();
      if (isMine) {
        if (typeof goProfile === 'function') goProfile();
      } else {
        if (typeof goCharacterProfile === 'function') goCharacterProfile(altId);
      }
    }, true);

    // 클릭 가능한 아바타에 커서 표시 (CSS 한 줄 주입)
    const style = document.createElement('style');
    style.textContent = `
      .post-avatar, .comment-avatar { cursor: pointer; }
      .dm-chat .post-avatar, .dm-chat .comment-avatar,
      .messenger-page .post-avatar, .messenger-page .comment-avatar { cursor: default; }
    `;
    document.head.appendChild(style);
  })();

</script>
</body>
</html>
```

```js
  // 모든 라우트에 trailing slash 적용 (legacy HTML의 상대 URL 해석을 위해 /app/는 슬래시 유지)
```

### `next/middleware.ts`  _(39 lines)_

```ts
import { NextResponse, type NextRequest } from "next/server";

const PUBLIC_PATHS = [
  "/",
  "/login",
  "/signup",
  "/verify-email",
  "/forgot-password",
  "/reset-password"
];

function isPublicPath(path: string): boolean {
  const normalized = path.endsWith("/") && path !== "/" ? path.slice(0, -1) : path;
  return PUBLIC_PATHS.includes(normalized);
}

export function middleware(req: NextRequest) {
  const path = req.nextUrl.pathname;

  if (isPublicPath(path)) return NextResponse.next();
  if (path.startsWith("/api/")) return NextResponse.next();
  if (path.startsWith("/_next/")) return NextResponse.next();
  if (path.startsWith("/legacy/")) return NextResponse.next();

  const cookie =
    req.cookies.get("__Secure-authjs.session-token") ||
    req.cookies.get("authjs.session-token");

  if (!cookie?.value) {
    const loginUrl = new URL("/login", req.url);
    loginUrl.searchParams.set("next", path);
    return NextResponse.redirect(loginUrl);
  }
  return NextResponse.next();
}

export const config = {
  matcher: ["/app/:path*", "/app"]
};
```


### `next/auth.config.ts`  _(35 lines)_

```ts
import type { NextAuthConfig } from "next-auth";

/**
 * Edge-safe Auth.js 설정 (PrismaAdapter 없음).
 * 미들웨어에서 import해서 사용. lib/auth.ts가 이걸 확장해서 providers/adapter를 더한다.
 */
export const authConfig: NextAuthConfig = {
  session: { strategy: "jwt" },
  trustHost: true,
  pages: {
    signIn: "/login",
    verifyRequest: "/verify-email",
    error: "/login"
  },
  callbacks: {
    authorized({ auth, request: { nextUrl } }) {
      const isLoggedIn = !!auth?.user;
      const path = nextUrl.pathname;
      const isAppRoute = path === "/app" || path.startsWith("/app/");
      if (isAppRoute) return isLoggedIn;
      return true;
    },
    jwt({ token, user }) {
      if (user) token.uid = (user as { id: string }).id;
      return token;
    },
    session({ session, token }) {
      if (token.uid && session.user) {
        (session.user as { id?: string }).id = token.uid as string;
      }
      return session;
    }
  },
  providers: []
};
```

### `next/lib/auth.ts`  _(37 lines)_

```ts
import NextAuth from "next-auth";
import Credentials from "next-auth/providers/credentials";
import { PrismaAdapter } from "@auth/prisma-adapter";
import bcrypt from "bcryptjs";
import { z } from "zod";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { authConfig } from "@/auth.config";

export const { handlers, auth, signIn, signOut } = NextAuth({
  ...authConfig,
  adapter: PrismaAdapter(prisma),
  providers: [
    Credentials({
      name: "credentials",
      credentials: {
        email: { label: "Email", type: "email" },
        password: { label: "Password", type: "password" }
      },
      authorize: async (creds) => {
        const parsed = z
          .object({ email: z.string().email(), password: z.string().min(8) })
          .safeParse(creds);
        if (!parsed.success) return null;

        const user = await prisma.user.findUnique({
          where: { email: parsed.data.email.toLowerCase() }
        });
        if (!user?.passwordHash) return null;

        const ok = await bcrypt.compare(parsed.data.password, user.passwordHash);
        if (!ok) return null;

        return { id: user.id, email: user.email, name: user.name ?? undefined };
      }
    })
  ]
});
```


### `next/lib/admin.ts`  _(22 lines)_

```ts
/**
 * 어드민 가드: 고정된 단일 관리자 이메일만 통과.
 */
import { auth } from "@/lib/auth";

const ADMIN_EMAIL = "wontyme88@naver.com";

export async function requireAdmin(): Promise<
  | { ok: true; userId: string; email: string }
  | { ok: false; status: 401 | 403 }
> {
  const session = await auth();
  const email = session?.user?.email;
  const userId = (session?.user as { id?: string } | undefined)?.id;
  if (!email || !userId) return { ok: false, status: 401 };

  if (email.toLowerCase() !== ADMIN_EMAIL) {
    return { ok: false, status: 403 };
  }

  return { ok: true, userId, email };
}
```


### `next/lib/crypto.ts`  _(43 lines)_

```ts
import { createCipheriv, createDecipheriv, randomBytes } from "node:crypto";

const ALGO = "aes-256-gcm";

function getKey(): Buffer {
  const raw = process.env.APP_ENCRYPTION_KEY;
  if (!raw) {
    throw new Error("APP_ENCRYPTION_KEY is not set");
  }
  const key = Buffer.from(raw, "base64");
  if (key.length !== 32) {
    throw new Error("APP_ENCRYPTION_KEY must decode to 32 bytes (256 bits)");
  }
  return key;
}

export function encrypt(plaintext: string): { ciphertext: string; iv: string; tag: string } {
  const iv = randomBytes(12);
  const cipher = createCipheriv(ALGO, getKey(), iv);
  const enc = Buffer.concat([cipher.update(plaintext, "utf8"), cipher.final()]);
  const tag = cipher.getAuthTag();
  return {
    ciphertext: enc.toString("base64"),
    iv: iv.toString("base64"),
    tag: tag.toString("base64")
  };
}

export function decrypt(ciphertext: string, iv: string, tag: string): string {
  const decipher = createDecipheriv(ALGO, getKey(), Buffer.from(iv, "base64"));
  decipher.setAuthTag(Buffer.from(tag, "base64"));
  const dec = Buffer.concat([
    decipher.update(Buffer.from(ciphertext, "base64")),
    decipher.final()
  ]);
  return dec.toString("utf8");
}

export function maskKey(key: string | null | undefined): string | null {
  if (!key) return null;
  if (key.length <= 8) return "****";
  return `${key.slice(0, 3)}…${key.slice(-4)}`;
}
```


### `next/lib/otp.ts`  _(25 lines)_

```ts
import bcrypt from "bcryptjs";
import crypto from "node:crypto";

export function generateOtp(): string {
  // 6자리, 0 패딩
  return Math.floor(100000 + Math.random() * 900000).toString();
}

export function hashOtp(code: string): Promise<string> {
  return bcrypt.hash(code, 10);
}

export function verifyOtp(code: string, hash: string): Promise<boolean> {
  return bcrypt.compare(code, hash);
}

export function generateResetToken(): { token: string; hash: string } {
  const token = crypto.randomBytes(32).toString("hex");
  const hash = crypto.createHash("sha256").update(token).digest("hex");
  return { token, hash };
}

export function hashResetToken(token: string): string {
  return crypto.createHash("sha256").update(token).digest("hex");
}
```

```ts
  : null;

const FROM = process.env.RESEND_FROM_EMAIL || "Princessgram <onboarding@resend.dev>";

function devLog(label: string, payload: Record<string, unknown>) {
  if (process.env.NODE_ENV !== "production") {
    console.log(`[email/dev] ${label}`, payload);
  }
}

export async function sendVerificationEmail(to: string, code: string) {
  devLog("verification", { to, code });
  if (!resend) return; // 키 없으면 콘솔 로그만
  await resend.emails.send({
    from: FROM,
    to,
    subject: "Princessgram 이메일 인증 코드",
    html: `
      <div style="font-family: 'Apple SD Gothic Neo', sans-serif; padding: 24px;">
        <h2 style="color:#ec4899">Princessgram 이메일 인증</h2>

      </div>
    `
  });
}

export async function sendPasswordResetEmail(to: string, link: string) {

  if (!resend) return;
  await resend.emails.send({
    from: FROM,
    to,
    subject: "Princessgram 비밀번호 재설정",
    html: `

        <h2 style="color:#ec4899">비밀번호 재설정</h2>
        <p>아래 링크를 눌러 30분 안에 새 비밀번호를 설정해주세요.</p>
        <p><a href="${link}" style="background:#ec4899;color:#fff;padding:10px 18px;border-radius:10px;text-decoration:none;display:inline-block;">비밀번호 재설정</a></p>
        <p style="color:#666; font-size: 12px;">본인이 요청하지 않았다면 이 메일을 무시하셔도 됩니다.</p>
      </div>
    `
  });
}
```
